	Section Pages_and_Dialog_Support {
		DeclarationSection Public_Pages_Support_Declarations {
			Set AllPageIds {
				Index: indexPageId;
				Comment: "This set contains the pageIds for pages of all types (see set AllPageTypes) that are created in WebUI.";
			}
			Set AllPageTypes {
				Index: apt;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'page', ! Regular pages are pages that appear in the Navigation Menu and can also be used as part of Workflows. To view all the pageIds for Regular pages see AllRegularPages.
					'pagev2-grid', ! Regular pages with Grid Layout.
					'dialog', ! Dialog pages are pages with pre-defined sizes(Small, Medium and Large) that are used for intermediate actions or steps in your application. To view all the pageIds for Dialog pages see AllDialogPages.
					'pagev2-grid-dialog', ! Dialog pages with Grid Layout.
					'sidepanel', ! Side Panels are 2 column width pages that can be configured and accessed on different/all pages in an application via tabs on the right-hand side of the page. To view all the pageIds for Side Panel pages see AllSidePanelPages.
					'pagev2-grid-sidepanel', ! Side Panels with Grid Layout
					'structural', ! Structural pages are pages that are used purely for AIMMS purposes, to create the path (see set PagePath) and URLs for pages in the application.
					'wizard' ! Wizard pages are pages that are made part of the Wizard in the WebUI.
					}
				}
				Comment: "This set defines all the types of pages available in WebUI.";
			}
			Set AllRegularPages {
				SubsetOf: AllPageIds;
				Index: arp;
				Definition: {
					{ indexPageId | PageType(indexPageId) = 'page' or PageType(indexPageId) = 'pagev2-grid' }
				}
				Comment: {
					"A subset of AllPageIds that includes pageIds for Regular pages only.
					Regular pages are pages that appear in the Navigation Menu and can also be used as part of Workflows.
					These pageIds are used as an argument in the OpenPage procedure and to configure steps of a Workflow in the WorkflowPageSpecification set.
					
					To identify which page is open in the WebUI, please check the data in webui::CurrentPageId which contains the pageIds for all open tabs."
				}
			}
			Set AllSidePanelPages {
				SubsetOf: AllPageIds;
				Index: aspp;
				Definition: {
					{ indexPageId | PageType(indexPageId) = 'sidepanel' or PageType(indexPageId) = 'pagev2-grid-sidepanel' }
				}
				Comment: {
					"A subset of AllPageIds that includes pageIds for Side Panel pages.
					These pageIds should be used to configure Side Panels in the SidePanelSpecification set.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/side-panels.html
					
					To identify which sidepanel tab is open in the WebUI, please check the data in webui::CurrentSidePanelPageId which contains the pageIds for the sidepanel tab that is open in the WebUI."
				}
			}
			Set AllDialogPages {
				SubsetOf: AllPageIds;
				Index: adp;
				Definition: {
					{ indexPageId | PageType(indexPageId) = 'dialog' or PageType(indexPageId) = 'pagev2-grid-dialog' }
				}
				Comment: {
					"A subset of AllPageIds that includes pageIds for Dialog pages.
					These pageIds are used as an argument in the OpenDialogPage procedure to invoke the respective Dialog page.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/dialog-pages.html"
				}
			}
			ElementParameter PageType {
				IndexDomain: indexPageId;
				Range: AllPageTypes;
				Comment: "An element parameter that maps each pageId (from the set AllPageIds) to its page type (from the set AllPageTypes).";
			}
			StringParameter PagePath {
				IndexDomain: indexPageId;
				Comment: "A string parameter that contains the path for each page created in the WebUI.";
			}
			StringParameter PageName {
				IndexDomain: indexPageId;
				Comment: "A string parameter that maps each pageId to the page name defined in the Page Manager in the WebUI.";
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection Public_Page_and_Widget_Specification_Declarations {
			Set SidePanelSpecification {
				Index: indexSidePanelSpec, indexTabSpec;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'displayText', ! (Optional) The text to be displayed on the tab on the Side Panel.
					'pageId', ! (REQUIRED) Side Panel pageId (from the AllSidePanelPages set).
					'tooltip', ! (Optional) Tooltip to be displayed when the user hovers over the respective tab.
					'icon', ! (Optional) The icon you want to display for the respective side panel tab.
					'iconcolor', ! (Optional) The color you want to display for the icon.
					'state' ! (Optional) Active (displayed and clickable) and Hidden (not displayed). By default, the state is Active. We suggest to add a state to the specification.
					}
				}
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure Side Panels on pages. You will need to create string parameters indexed over this set.
					The string parameters configured should be added in the respective page\'s Page Settings -> Page Extensions section.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/side-panels.html"
				}
			}
			Set WidgetActionSpecification {
				Index: indexWidgetActionSpec, indexInlineActionSpec;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'displaytext', ! (Optional) The text/label you would like to give the action.
					'tooltip', ! (Optional) A tooltip that appears when the user hovers over the respective action. This is only applicable when using the "Inline Actions" experimental feature of the List widget.
					'icon', ! (Optional) The icon you want to display for the respective action.
					'procedure', ! (Optional) The procedure you want to call when the respective action is clicked.
					'state', ! (Optional) Active (displayed and clickable), Inactive (displayed and not clickable) and Hidden (not displayed). By default, the state is Hidden. 
					'actiontype' ! (Optional) (Case-Insensitive) The type of action performed in accordance to the procedure specified. Can hold the values: Procedure (Default behavior. The procedure alone is executed.), Upload (File selection operation followed by the procedure execution), Download (Procedure execution followed by File save operation.) If not defined, actiontype is regarded as a Procedure type.
					}
				}
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure Widget Actions on widgets. You will need to create string parameters indexed over this set.
					
					The string parameters configured should be added in the respective widget\'s Widget Settings -> Widget Actions section.
					
					Widget Actions are available for the following widgets:
					1. Table
					2. Bar Chart
					3. Line Chart
					4. Pie Chart
					5. Tree Map Chart
					6. Gantt Chart
					7. Bubble Chart
					8. Map
					9. Multiselect
					10. Scalar (EXCEPT for the compact mode)
					11. Legend
					12. Slider
					13. List
					
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/widget-options.html#widget-actions"
				}
			}
			Set PageActionSpecification {
				Index: indexPageActionSpec;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'displaytext', ! (Optional) The text/label you would like to give the action.
					'icon', ! (Optional) The icon you want to display for the respective action.
					'procedure', ! (Optional) The procedure you want to call when the respective action is clicked.
					'state' ! (Optional) Active (displayed and clickable), Inactive (displayed and not clickable) and Hidden (not displayed). By default, the state is Hidden.
					}
				}
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure the Primary Action and Secondary Actions on pages. You will need to create string parameters indexed over this set.
					The string parameters configured should be added in the respective page\'s Page Settings -> Page Extensions section.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/page-settings.html#page-actions"
				}
			}
			Set WidgetItemActionSpecification {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				Index: indexWidgetItemActionSpec;
				Definition: AllSets + AllParameters + AllVariables + AllIndices;
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure Item Actions in widgets. You will need to create string parameters indexed over this set.
					The string parameters configured should be added in the respective widget\'s Widget Settings -> Widget Actions -> Item Actions option."
				}
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection Public_Workflow_Support_Declarations {
			Set WorkflowPageSpecifications {
				SubsetOf: AllWebUIProperties;
				Index: indexWorkflowPageSpec;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'displayText', ! (Optional) Text to be displayed for the step in the Workflow Panel.
					'icon', ! (Optional) The icon you want to display for the respective step.
					'pageId', ! (REQUIRED) The pageId of Regular Pages (from the AllRegularPages set) this step should be associated with. Ideally, every page in a Workflow is a step in the Workflow Panel.
					'tooltip', ! (Optional) Tooltip to be displayed when the user hovers over the respective step.
					'workflowPageState', ! (Optional) The state for a step in the Workflow Panel. Can hold the values: Active (displayed and clickable), Inactive (displayed and not clickable) or Hidden (not displayed). By default, the state is Hidden.
					'pageDataState', ! (Optional) The data state of the step. Can hold the vaules: Complete (indicated with a tick mark on the step), Incomplete (indicated with blue colour) or Error (indicated with red colour). If not defined, by default it has an Empty state (the step has no colour).
					'redirectPageId', ! (Optional) The pageId (from the AllRegularPages set) of the page the user should be redirected to when the workflowPageState is Inactive or Hidden.
					'parentPageId' ! (Optional) The pageId of a Workflow step, which enables this step to be presented as a child of that step.
					}
				}
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure the number of steps/pages in a specific Workflow. You will need to create a string parameter indexed over this set.
					The string parameter configured should be added in the Application Settings -> Workflow Panel -> Workflow Steps field.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/application-settings.html#workflow-panel"
				}
			}
			Set WorkflowSpecifications {
				Index: indexWorkflowSpec;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'title' , ! (Optional) The title for the Workflow to be displayed on top of the Workflow Panel.
					'style' ! (CURRENTLY NOT IN USE) When styles or themes are introduced for the Workflow Panel, this attribute will hold that value.
					}
				}
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure the number of Workflows in the application. You will need to create a string parameter indexed over this set.
					The string parameter configured should be added in the Application Settings -> Workflow Panel -> Workflow field.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/application-settings.html#workflow-panel"
				}
			}
			Set ExtensionOrder {
				SubsetOf: Integers;
				Index: indexWorkflowOrder,indexNoOfPages,indexPageExtension,indexApplicationExtension,indexWidgetExtension,indexListGroupOrder,indexNoOfListItems,indexNoOfTabs;
				Parameter: CurrentWorkflow, CurrentWorkflowStep;
				Property: ElementsAreNumerical;
				Definition: data {1..100};
				Comment: {
					"A subset of the pre-declared set Integers, with several indices:
					indexWorkflowOrder, used to reference the number of Workflows.
					indexNoOfPages, used to reference the number of pages or steps in each Workflow.
					indexPageExtension, used to reference the number of page extension such as Page Actions, Side Panels and Widget Actions.
					indexApplicationExtension, used to reference the number of application extension such as Status Bar messages.
					indexWidgetExtension, used to reference the number of widget extensions such as Widget Actions.
					indexListOrder, used to reference the number of list groups.
					indexNoOfListItems, used for defining number of list items per list group.
					
					indexWorkflowOrder and indexNoOfPages are used as dimensions of the string parameters which will configure the Workflows and the steps of the Workflows in the application.
					indexPageExtension is used as a dimension of the string parameter which will configure the Page Actions(Primary and Secondary), Side Panels and Widget Actions on pages and widgets respectively.
					indexApplicationExtension is used as a dimension of the string parameter which will configure the Status Bar messages in the application.
					indexWidgetExtension is used as a dimension of the string parameter which will configure the Widget Actions in a widget.
					indexListOrder and indexNoOfListItems are used as dimensions of the string parameters which will configure the Lists and the List Items in the List widget.
					
					The element parameters CurrentWorkflow and CurrentWorkflowStepdefined are used to identify the current Workflow and the current Workflow step respectively, that the user is on. (NOTE: This cannot be used currently, and the element parameters hold no values)
					
					Documentation:
					Workflows: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/application-settings.html#workflow-panel
					Side Panels: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/page-manager.html#sidepanels
					Page Actions: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/page-settings.html#page-actions
					Widget Actions: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/widget-options.html#widget-actions
					Status Bar: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/application-settings.html#status-bar
					List Widget: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/list-widget.html"
				}
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection Public_StatusBar_Support_Declarations {
			Set StatusBarSpecification {
				Index: indexStatusBarSpec;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'header', ! (Optional) The Header/Title text you want to display for the status message.
					'icon', ! (Optional) The icon you want to display for the respective message.
					'color', ! (Optional) The color you want to display for the icon.
					'text', ! (REQUIRED) The text you want to display for the status message.
					'tooltip', ! (Optional) Tooltip to be displayed when the user hovers over the respective status message.
					'procedure', ! (Optional) The procedure you want to call when the respective status message is clicked.
					'state' ! (Optional) Active (displayed and clickable), Inactive (displayed and not clickable) and Hidden (not displayed). By default, the state is Hidden.
					}
				}
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure Status Messages on the Status Bar that appears on the footer. You will need to create string parameters indexed over this set.
					The string parameters configured should be added in the Application Settings -> Application Extensions -> StatusBar Messages.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/status-bar.html"
				}
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection Public_List_Support_Declarations {
			Set ListGroupSpecification {
				Index: indexListGroupSpec;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'title', ! (Optional) The title for the list group to be displayed in the List widget.
					'tooltip' ! (Optional) Tooltip to be displayed when the user hovers over the respective list group's title.
					}
				}
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure the number of group of lists to be displayed in the List widget. You will need to create a string parameter indexed over this set.
					The string parameter configured should be added in the respective list widget\'s Widget Settings -> List settings -> List Groups.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/list-widget.html"
				}
			}
			Set ListGroupItemsSpecification {
				Index: indexListGroupItemsSpec;
				Definition: {
					data {
					'icon', ! (Optional) The icon you want to display for the respective list item.
					'color', ! (Optional) The color you want to display for the icon.
					'text', ! (REQUIRED) The text you want to display for the list item.
					'tooltip', ! (Optional) Tooltip to be displayed when the user hovers over the respective list item.
					'procedure', ! (Optional) The procedure you want to call when the respective list item is clicked.
					'external', ! (Optional) Boolean value to indicate the list item procedure leads to an external link. By default, this option is 0/false.
					'state' ! (Optional) Active (displayed and clickable), Inactive (displayed and not clickable) and Hidden (not displayed). By default, the state is Hidden.
					}
				}
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to configure the list items in a specific list group. You will need to create a string parameter indexed over this set.
					The string parameter configured should be added in the respective list widget\'s Widget Settings -> List settings -> List Items.
					Documentation: https://manual.aimms.com/webui/list-widget.html"
				}
			}
		}

			Procedure OpenSidePanel {
				Arguments: (pageId);
				Body: {
					if (Card(Requests) >= 1) then
						raise error "WebUI cannot handle page requests, when there are still other unhandled browser requests";
					endif;
					
					if (not pageId) then
						raise error "pageId cannot be empty when opening a side panel";
					endif;
					
					RequestCounter += 1;
					Requests += RequestCounter;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'id') := FormatString("%n",RequestCounter);

					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'result') := "<empty>";
					
					LocalLogInfo(FormatString("OpenSidePanel: RequestQueue type=SidePanel | id=%n | pageId=%s", RequestCounter, pageId));

		Section Public_Dialog_Support_Procedures {
			Procedure RequestPerformWebUIDialog {
				Arguments: (title,message,actions,onDone);
				Body: {
					NumberOfConcurrentRequests := Count(rq | RequestQueue(rq,'type') <> "UploadFile" and RequestQueue(rq,'type') <> "DownloadFile" and RequestQueue(rq,'type') <> "ShowNotification");
					if (NumberOfConcurrentRequests >= 1) then
						raise error "WebUI cannot open dialogs, when there are still other unhandled browser requests";
					endif;
					
					RequestCounter += 1;
					Requests += RequestCounter;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'id') := FormatString("%n",RequestCounter);
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'title') := title;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'type') := "MessageDialog";
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'message') := message;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'actions') := SettoJsonList(actions);
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'onDone') := onDone;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'result') := "<empty>";
					
					LocalLogInfo(FormatString("RequestPerformWebUIDialog: RequestQueue type=MessageDialog | id=%n | title=%s", RequestCounter, title));
					
					return RequestCounter;
				}
				Comment: {
					"The procedure webui::requestPerformWebUIDialog displays a message dialog in a WebUI page.
					Along with the message we can also display buttons that are bound to custom actions.
					
					Arguments:
					title: A string parameter which contains the text to be displayed as the title of the dialog box.
					message: A string parameter which contains the message to be displayed in the dialog box.
					actions: A set of custom actions. The elements of this set are represented as buttons in the message dialog and their text is the same as the action names. When an action is selected (button is clicked), it invokes the onDone procedure with the corresponding action as an argument.
					onDone: A reference to a procedure in the set AllProcedures. The procedure should have a single input string parameter as argument. When a user selects an action, the onDone procedure is invoked with the action name as its argument.
					
					Remarks:
					- When you just want to send a message to the user, you should provide a single action (e.g. Actions := {\'OK\'}) and you can use \'\' for the onDone argument. In this case, no procedure is called and the user can just close the \'dialog\' by pressing the single action (or pressing the return/space key, which will press the default (last, highlighted) button).
					- You can use a translation file (e.g. ‘WebUI/resources/languages/<dialog_actions>.properties’) to provide translations for the various internal action names, containing, for example: discard-and-continue = Discard and continue"
				}
				Set actions {
					Index: a;
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter title {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter message {
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter onDone {
					Range: AllProcedures;
					Default: 'webui::NoOp1';
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter NumberOfConcurrentRequests;
			}
			Procedure OpenDialogPage {
				Arguments: (pageId,title,actions,onDone);
				Body: {
					NumberOfConcurrentRequests := Count(rq | RequestQueue(rq,'type') <> "UploadFile" and RequestQueue(rq,'type') <> "DownloadFile" and RequestQueue(rq,'type') <> "ShowNotification");
					if (NumberOfConcurrentRequests >= 1) then
						raise error "WebUI cannot open dialogs, when there are still other unhandled browser requests";
					endif;
					
					if (not pageId) then
						raise error "pageId cannot be empty when opening a dialog page";
					endif;
					
					RequestCounter += 1;
					Requests += RequestCounter;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'id') := FormatString("%n",RequestCounter);
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'title') := title;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'type') := "DialogPage";
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'pageId') := pageId;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'actions') := SettoJsonList(actions);
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'onDone') := onDone;
					RequestQueue(RequestCounter, 'result') := "<empty>";
					
					LocalLogInfo(FormatString("OpenDialogPage: RequestQueue type=DialogPage | id=%n | title=%s", RequestCounter, title));
					
					return RequestCounter;
				}
				Comment: {
					"The procedure webui::OpenDialogPage opens a user-specified page in a modal manner in the WebUI
					Along with opening the page you can also display buttons that are bound to custom actions.
					
					Arguments:
					pageId: the pageId of the page you want to be opened as a modal dialog page.
					title: A string parameter which contains the text to be displayed as the title of the dialog box.
					actions: A set of custom actions. The elements of this set are represented as buttons in the message dialog and their text is the same as the action names. When an action is selected (button is clicked), it invokes the onDone procedure with the corresponding action as an argument.
					onDone: A reference to a procedure in the set AllProcedures. The procedure should have a single input string parameter as argument. When a user selects an action, the onDone procedure is invoked with the action name as its argument."
				}
				Set actions {
					Index: a;
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter title {
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter pageId {
					Range: AllDialogPages;
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter onDone {
					Range: AllProcedures;
					Default: 'webui::NoOp1';
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter NumberOfConcurrentRequests;
			}
			Procedure IsWebUIDialogOpen {
				Body: {
					return (Card(Requests) >= 1);
				}
				Comment: "The webui::IsWebUIDialogOpen procedure returns 1 if a dialog is open or displayed on WebUI, else it returns 0.";
			}
		}

		Procedure InitializeModelWebUIInteraction {
			Body: {
				if(not requestQueueMonitor) then
					monitoredIdentifiers := { 'RequestQueue' };
					DataChangeMonitorRegisterCallback(monitoredIdentifiers, 'webui::HandleCompletedRequests', requestQueueMonitor);
				endif;
			}
			Set monitoredIdentifiers {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
		Procedure HandleCompletedRequests {
			Body: {
				CompletedRequests := { rq | RequestQueue(rq, 'result') <> "<empty>" };
				
				while Card(CompletedRequests) > 0 do
					completedRequest := First(CompletedRequests);
					requestType := RequestQueue(completedRequest, 'type');
					isUploadFileRequest := 1 onlyif (requestType = "UploadFile");
					if (isUploadFileRequest) then 
						onDone3 := StringToElement(AllProcedures, RequestQueue(completedRequest,'onDone'));
					else
						onDone1 := StringToElement(AllProcedures, RequestQueue(completedRequest,'onDone'));
					endif;
					resultValue := RequestQueue(completedRequest, 'result');
				
					LocalLogInfo(
						FormatString("HandleCompletedRequests: RequestQueue resultValue=%s | type=%s | id=%s",
							resultValue,
							RequestQueue(completedRequest, 'type'),
							RequestQueue(completedRequest, 'id')
						)
					);
				
					empty RequestQueue(completedRequest,df);
					Requests -= completedRequest;
					if (isUploadFileRequest) then
						if (onDone3) then
							LocalLogInfo(
								FormatString("HandleCompletedRequests: calling onDone for RequestQueue onDone=%s | resultValue=%s | type=%s | id=%s",
									onDone3,
									resultValue,
									RequestQueue(completedRequest, 'type'),
									RequestQueue(completedRequest, 'id')
								)
							);
							Apply(onDone3, resultValue, statusCode, statusDescription);
						endif;
					else
						if (onDone1) then
							LocalLogInfo(
								FormatString("HandleCompletedRequests: calling onDone for RequestQueue onDone=%s | resultValue=%s | type=%s | id=%s",
									onDone1,
									resultValue,
									RequestQueue(completedRequest, 'type'),
									RequestQueue(completedRequest, 'id')
								)
							);
							Apply(onDone1, resultValue);
						endif;
					endif;
				endwhile;
			}
			ElementParameter completedRequest {
				Range: Requests;
			}
			Set CompletedRequests {
				SubsetOf: Requests;
				Index: crq;
			}
			StringParameter requestType;
			Parameter isUploadFileRequest;
			ElementParameter onDone1 {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp1';
			}
			ElementParameter onDone3 {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp3';
			}
			Parameter statusCode;
			StringParameter statusDescription;
			StringParameter resultValue;
			ElementParameter err {
				Range: errh::PendingErrors;
			}
		}
	}
	Section Timezone_Support {
		Section Public_Timezone_Support_Procedures {
			Procedure DetermineTimeZoneData {
				Body: {
					empty DSTSettings, TimeZoneOffset;
					
					OptionGetValue('Current_Time_in_LocalDST',OptionValueLower,OptionValueCurrent,OptionValueDefault,OptionValueUpper);
					! Temporaily set 'Current_Time_in_LocalDST' to 'Off'
					OptionSetValue('Current_Time_in_LocalDST',0);
					CurrentTimeLocalNoDST := CurrentToString("%c%y-%m-%d %H:%M");
					! Restore 'Current_Time_in_LocalDST' to its original value
					OptionSetValue('Current_Time_in_LocalDST',OptionValueCurrent);
					
					CurrentTimeUTC := ConvertReferenceDate(CurrentTimeLocalNoDST, 'Local', 'UTC', ignoreDST: 1);
					
					for (indexAllDisplayTimeZones) do
					
						if ( HasDifferentRepresentationWhenComparingWithAndWithoutDST(DateInJune, indexAllDisplayTimeZones) ) then
							! timezone has DST set in June
							DSTSettings(indexAllDisplayTimeZones) := 1;
						elseif ( HasDifferentRepresentationWhenComparingWithAndWithoutDST(DateInDecember, indexAllDisplayTimeZones) ) then
							! timezone has DST set in December
							DSTSettings(indexAllDisplayTimeZones) := 1;
						endif;
					
						AppIgnoreDST := 1 - DSTSettings(indexAllDisplayTimeZones);
						AppTime := ConvertReferenceDate(CurrentTimeLocalNoDST, 'Local', indexAllDisplayTimeZones, ignoreDST: AppIgnoreDST);
						Offset := StringToMoment("%c%y-%m-%d %H:%M%TZ('UTC')", [minute], CurrentTimeUTC, AppTime);
						OffsetInMinutes := Offset / ((1) [minute]);
					
						! Offset contains the offset between 'Local' and 'UTC'
						TimeZoneOffset(indexAllDisplayTimeZones) := Round(OffsetInMinutes);
					
					endfor;
					
					! Transpose to make offset for UTC equal to 0
					TimeZoneOffset(indexAllDisplayTimeZones) -= TimeZoneOffset('UTC');
					
					Factor := 1;
					UTC12 := StringToElement(AllDisplayTimeZones,"UTC+12");
					if (UTC12) then
						! In some model the call to 'StringToMoment' (line 18) return an answer in hour :(
						Factor := 720 / TimeZoneOffset(UTC12);
					endif;
					
					for (indexAllDisplayTimeZones) do
						! Make sure the offset contains the number of *minutes*
						TimeZoneOffset(indexAllDisplayTimeZones) *= Factor;
					endfor;
				}
				StringParameter DateInDecember {
					InitialData: {
						"2019-12-01 00:00";
					}
				}
				StringParameter DateInJune {
					InitialData: {
						"2019-06-01 00:00";
					}
				}
				StringParameter CurrentTimeLocalNoDST;
				StringParameter CurrentTimeUTC;
				Parameter AppIgnoreDST;
				StringParameter AppTime;
				Parameter Offset {
					Unit: minute;
				}
				Parameter OffsetInMinutes;
				ElementParameter UTC12 {
					Range: AllDisplayTimeZones;
				}
				Parameter Factor;
				Parameter OptionValueLower;
				Parameter OptionValueCurrent;
				Parameter OptionValueDefault;
				Parameter OptionValueUpper;
			}
			Function HasDifferentRepresentationWhenComparingWithAndWithoutDST {
				Arguments: (dateValue,tmZone);
				Body: {
					TimeDST := ConvertReferenceDate(dateValue, 'Local', tmZone, ignoreDST: 0);
					TimeNoDST := ConvertReferenceDate(dateValue, 'Local', tmZone, ignoreDST: 1);
					HasDifferentRepresentationWhenComparingWithAndWithoutDST := if ( TimeDST <> TimeNoDST ) then 1 else 0 endif;
				}
				StringParameter TimeNoDST;
				StringParameter TimeDST;
				StringParameter dateValue {
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter tmZone {
					Range: AllTimeZones;
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Procedure InitializeTimeZoneData {
				Body: {
					webui::DisplayTimeZones := webui::AllDisplayTimeZones;
					
					! Initialize sort order
					ActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(indexAllDisplayTimeZones) :=  LocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(indexAllDisplayTimeZones);
					
					DetermineTimeZoneData;
					Success := 1;
					
					update WebApplicationTimeZone;
					
					for (indexAllDisplayTimeZones) do
						TimeZoneNameWithoutOffset := LocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(indexAllDisplayTimeZones);
						ClosingBracketPos := FindString(TimeZoneNameWithoutOffset,")");
						if (ClosingBracketPos) then
							TimeZoneNameWithoutOffset := SubString(TimeZoneNameWithoutOffset,ClosingBracketPos+1,StringLength(TimeZoneNameWithoutOffset));
						endif;
						Offset := TimeZoneOffset(indexAllDisplayTimeZones);
						if (Offset >= 0) then
							IsPositive := 1;
						else
							IsPositive := 0;
						endif;
						HourOffset := Div(Abs(Offset),60);
						MinuteOffset := Mod(Abs(Offset),60);
						TimeZoneNameWithOffset := "(UTC";
						if (indexAllDisplayTimeZones <> 'UTC') then
							TimeZoneNameWithOffset += (if (IsPositive) then "+" else "-" endif);
							TimeZoneNameWithOffset += FormatString("%02i",HourOffset);
							TimeZoneNameWithOffset += ":";
							TimeZoneNameWithOffset += FormatString("%02i",Round(MinuteOffset));
						endif;
						TimeZoneNameWithOffset += ")";
						TimeZoneNameWithOffset += TimeZoneNameWithoutOffset;
						TempActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(indexAllDisplayTimeZones) := TimeZoneNameWithOffset;
					endfor;
					
					if (Success) then
						ActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(indexAllDisplayTimeZones) := TempActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(indexAllDisplayTimeZones);
					else
						raise warning "Unable to compute correct UTC offsets";
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter TimeZoneNameWithoutOffset;
				StringParameter TimeZoneNameWithOffset;
				Parameter Offset;
				Parameter HourOffset;
				Parameter MinuteOffset;
				Parameter IsPositive;
				Parameter ClosingBracketPos;
				Parameter Success;
				StringParameter TempActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName {
					IndexDomain: indexAllDisplayTimeZones;
				}
			}
			Procedure UponChange_DisplayTimeZone {
				Body: {
					if (TimeZoneChangeHook) then
						apply(TimeZoneChangeHook);
					endif;
				}
				Comment: "This procedure is invoked when the value of DisplayTimeZone changes when the Application Time Zone changes in the WebUI, which inturn invokes the procedure set in TimeZoneChangeHook.";
			}
			Procedure UponChange_IgnoreDST {
				Body: {
					if (TimeZoneChangeHook) then
						apply(TimeZoneChangeHook);
					endif;
				}
				Comment: "This procedure is invoked when the value of IgnoreDST changes when the Consider daylight saving time switch changes in the WebUI, which inturn invokes the procedure set in TimeZoneChangeHook.";
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection Public_TimeZone_Support_Declarations {
			StringParameter LocaleTimeZoneDisplayNameForBrowserTimeZone {
				Definition: {
					LocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(webui::MappedBrowserTimeZone);
				}
				Comment: "This string parameter holds the value of the LocaleTimeZoneDisplayName that maps to the current user\'s browser time zone.";
			}
			Parameter TimeZoneOffset {
				IndexDomain: indexAllDisplayTimeZones;
			}
			Parameter DSTSettings {
				IndexDomain: indexAllDisplayTimeZones;
				Range: binary;
				Default: 0;
				Comment: "This binary parameter is used to determine if a time zone has daylight saving time or not.";
			}
			Parameter DefaultDSTSettingForDisplayTimeZone {
				Range: binary;
				Definition: {
					DSTSettings(webui::DisplayTimeZone);
				}
				Comment: "This binary parameter is used to determine if the daylight savings switch in the Time Zone Panel should be read-only for the selected webui::DisplayTimeZone.";
			}
			StringParameter BrowserTimeZoneMappingData {
				IndexDomain: (indexAllDisplayTimeZones in AllTimeZones,indexBrowser);

			StringParameter BrowserTimeZoneMapping {
				IndexDomain: (indexAllDisplayTimeZones in AllTimeZones,indexBrowser);
				Definition: {
					if (BrowserTimeZoneMappingData(indexAllDisplayTimeZones,indexBrowser)) then
						BrowserTimeZoneMappingData(indexAllDisplayTimeZones,indexBrowser)
					else
						FormatString("%e", indexAllDisplayTimeZones)
					endif;
				}
				Comment: "This string parameter contains the mapping of time zones returned from different browsers and the time zones available in AIMMS.";
			}
			ElementParameter MappedBrowserTimeZone {
				Range: AllDisplayTimeZones;
				Definition: first(webui::indexAllDisplayTimeZones | webui::BrowserTimeZoneMapping(webui::indexAllDisplayTimeZones, webui::ClientBrowserName) = webui::BrowserTimeZone);
				Comment: "This element parameter holds the value of the AIMMS time zone that maps to the current user\'s browser time zone, from the webui::BrowserTimeZoneMapping identifier.";
			}
			StringParameter ActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName {
				IndexDomain: indexAllDisplayTimeZones;
			}
			Set AllDisplayTimeZones {
				SubsetOf: AllTimeZones;
				Index: indexAllDisplayTimeZones;
				OrderBy: ActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(IndexTimeZones);
				Definition: AllTimeZones - {'Local','LocalDST'};
				Comment: "This set is used to define all the Time Zones that users can select in the WebUI, except Local and LocalDST which is used for AIMMS references.";
				webui::ElementTextIdentifier: ActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName;
			}
			Set DisplayTimeZones {
				SubsetOf: AllDisplayTimeZones;
				OrderBy: ActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName(IndexTimeZones);
				Comment: {
					"This set is used to display time zones with their respective offsets in the WebUI. eg: (UTC+01:00) Amsterdam, Berlin, Bern, Rome, Stockholm, Vienna.
					Model developers can initialize this set with just specific time zones if required. When initializing, the time zones should be from the AllDisplayTimeZones set."
				}
				webui::ElementTextIdentifier: ActualLocaleTimeZoneDisplayName;
			}
			ElementParameter DisplayTimeZone {
				Range: DisplayTimeZones;
				InitialData: '';
				Comment: "An element parameter ranged over the DisplayTimeZones set, used to set the time zone for the application via the Time Zone Panel.";
			}
			ElementParameter WebApplicationTimeZone {
				Range: AllTimeZones;
				Definition: {
					if (DisplayTimeZone) then
						DisplayTimeZone
					else
						'Local'
					endif
				}
				Comment: {
					"This element parameter holds the value of the time zone that is set in the Application Time Zone (webui::DisplayTimeZone) in the Time Zone Panel. If webui::DisplayTimeZone is not initialized and is empty, ApplicationTimeZone is assigned the Local time zone where the application is hosted.
					When configuring a calendar or string parameter that represents data and time, webui::WebApplicationTimeZone should be used in defining the Timeslot format and webui::DateTimeFormatIdentifier attributes respectively."
				}
			}
			ElementParameter ModelTimeZone {
				Range: AllTimeZones;
				InitialData: 'Local';
			}
			Parameter IgnoreDST {
				Range: binary;
				Default: 0;
				Comment: "A binary parameter that is used to toggle daylights saving time for the selected time zone in webui::DisplayTimeZone in the Time Zone Panel.";
			}
			StringParameter ApplicationHourlyTimeSlotFormat {
				Definition: {
					if webui::IgnoreDST then
						"%c%y-%m-%d %H %TZ(webui::WebApplicationTimeZone)"
					else
						"%c%y-%m-%d %H %TZ(webui::WebApplicationTimeZone)|\"\"|\" DST\"|"
					endif;
				}
				Comment: "This string parameter can be used in the Timeslot format in a calendar or webui::DateTimeFormatIdentifier in a string parameter to display the time format with or without \"DST\" based on the IgnoreDST setting, when the time is being displayed upto hours.";
			}
			StringParameter ApplicationMinuteTimeSlotFormat {
				Definition: {
					if webui::IgnoreDST then
						"%c%y-%m-%d %H:%M %TZ(webui::WebApplicationTimeZone)"
					else
						"%c%y-%m-%d %H:%M %TZ(webui::WebApplicationTimeZone)|\"\"|\" DST\"|"
					endif;
				}
				Comment: "This string parameter can be used in the Timeslot format in a calendar or webui::DateTimeFormatIdentifier in a string parameter to display the time format with or without \"DST\" based on the IgnoreDST setting, when the time is being displayed upto minutes.";
			}
			ElementParameter TimeZoneChangeHook {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp';
				InitialData: '';
				Comment: "This element parameter is ranged over AllProcedures. As an application developer you can invoke any procedures when the value of webui::DisplayTimeZone or webui::IgnoreDST is changed in the WebUI.";
			}
			Set ClientBrowsers {
				Index: indexBrowser;
				Definition: data {isChrome, isIE, isEdge, isSafari, isFirefox};
				Comment: {
					"This set includes a list of client browers and will be used to reference the Time Zone on the clients machine or the Local Time Zone.
					The element parameter ClientBrowserName is used to initialize the users browser when the application is launched."
				}
			}
			ElementParameter ClientBrowserName {
				Range: ClientBrowsers;
				InitialData: '';
			}
			StringParameter ClientBrowserLanguage {
				InitialData: {
					"";
				}
				Comment: "This string parameter is used to set the user\'s browser language when the application is launched.";
			}
			StringParameter BrowserTimeZone {
				InitialData: {
					"";
				}
				Comment: "This string parameter is used to set the user\'s browser time zone when the application is launched.";
			}
			ElementParameter ApplicationConvention {
				Range: AllConventions;

			Procedure IdentifierHasBinaryRange {
				Arguments: (idRef);
				Body: {
					block
						idName := FormatString("%e",idRef);
						return IdentifierHasBinaryRange_Internal(idName);
					onerror err do
							errh::MarkAsHandled(err);
					endblock;
					
					return 0;
				}
				ElementParameter idRef {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter err {
					Range: errh::PendingErrors;
				}
				StringParameter idName;
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection CubeEngineLinkDLL_Declarations {
			ElementParameter NativeUnicodeEncoding {
				Range: AllCharacterEncodings;
				Definition: {
					if (AimmsStringConstants('Platform')="Linux") then 'UTF-32LE' else 'UTF-16LE' endif;
				}
				Comment: "Because of a bug in earlier 3.14 versions, the specification of the default value is important here.";
			}
			StringParameter CubeEngineLinkDLLPath {
				Definition: if (AimmsStringConstants('Platform') = "Windows") then  DLLPath("WebUI", "CubeEngineLink") else  DLLPath("WebUI", "libCubeEngineLink") endif;
			}
			StringParameter CubeEngineDLLPath {
				Definition: if (AimmsStringConstants('Platform') = "Windows") then  DLLPath("WebUI", "CubeEngine") else  DLLPath("WebUI", "libCubeEngine") endif;
			}
			StringParameter WebUILibraryPath {
				Definition: LibraryDirectory("WebUI");
			}

			}

		Section CubeEngineLinkDLL_Utility_Functions {
			Function LibraryDirectory {
				Arguments: libraryName;
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					if DirectoryOfLibraryProject(libraryName, LibraryDirectory) <> 1 then
						LibraryDirectory := libraryName;
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter libraryName {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function DLLDirectory {
				Arguments: libraryName;
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					DLLDirectory := LibraryDirectory(libraryName) + "DLL" + if (AimmsStringConstants('Platform') = "Windows") then "\\" else "/" endif;
				}
				StringParameter libraryName {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function DLLPath {
				Arguments: (libraryName,dllName);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					DLLPath := DLLDirectory(libraryName) + dllName + if (AimmsStringConstants('Platform') = "Windows") then ".dll" else ".so" endif;
				}
				StringParameter libraryName {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter dllName {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
		}
		Procedure GenerateAllPublicIdentifiersSet {
			Body: {
				GetOrCreateImplicitPublicIdentifiers(setRef);
				GetOrCreateDefaultPublicIdentifiers(setRef);
				GetOrCreateExplicitPublicIdentifiers(setRef);
				
				GetOrCreateWebUIRuntimeLibrary(libRef);
				CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent("AllActualPublicIdentifiers",'set',libRef);
				
				GetRuntimeIdentifierRef("AllActualPublicIdentifiers",setRef);
				if (setRef) then
					me::SetAttribute(setRef,'subset of',"AllIdentifiers");
					me::SetAttribute(setRef,'definition',"webui_runtime::AllImplicitPublicIdentifiers + webui_runtime::AllExplicitPublicIdentifiers");
				endif;
				me::Compile(setRef);
				me::Compile(libref);
			}
			ElementParameter setRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter libRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
		Procedure GetOrCreateImplicitPublicIdentifiers {
			Arguments: (setRef);
			Body: {
				GetOrCreateWebUIRuntimeLibrary(libRef);
				
				CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent("AllImplicitPublicIdentifiers",'set',libRef);
				GetRuntimeIdentifierRef("AllImplicitPublicIdentifiers",setRef);
				if (setRef) then
					me::SetAttribute(setRef,'subset of',"AllIdentifiers");
					me::Compile(setRef);
				endif;
			}
			ElementParameter setRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Output;
			}
			ElementParameter libRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
		Procedure GetOrCreateExplicitPublicIdentifiers {
			Arguments: (setRef);
			Body: {
				GetOrCreateWebUIRuntimeLibrary(libRef);
				
				CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent("AllExplicitPublicIdentifiers",'set',libRef);
				GetRuntimeIdentifierRef("AllExplicitPublicIdentifiers",setRef);
				if (setRef) then
					me::SetAttribute(setRef,'subset of',"AllIdentifiers");
				
					if ( StringToElement(AllIdentifiers,"AllPublicIdentifiers") ) then
						me::SetAttribute( setRef,'definition',"::AllPublicIdentifiers" );
					else
						me::SetAttribute( setRef,'definition',"AllDefaultPublicIdentifiers" );
					endif;
				
					me::Compile(setRef);
				endif;
			}
			ElementParameter setRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Output;
			}
			ElementParameter libRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
		Procedure GetOrCreateDefaultPublicIdentifiers {
			Arguments: (setRef);
			Body: {
				GetOrCreateWebUIRuntimeLibrary(libRef);
				
				CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent("AllDefaultPublicIdentifiers",'set',libRef);
				GetRuntimeIdentifierRef("AllDefaultPublicIdentifiers",setRef);
				if (setRef) then
					me::SetAttribute(setRef,'subset of',"AllIdentifiers");
				endif;
				
				CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent("AllAllowedIdentifierTypes",'set',libRef);
				GetRuntimeIdentifierRef("AllAllowedIdentifierTypes",setRef);
				if (setRef) then
					me::SetAttribute(setRef,'subset of',"AllIdentifierTypes");
					me::SetAttribute(setRef,'definition',
						"data { set, calendar, horizon, index, parameter, 'element parameter',\n" +
						"    'string parameter', variable, 'element variable', 'complementarity variable',\n" +
						"    constraint, arc, node, 'uncertainty variable', 'uncertainty constraint',\n" +
						"    procedure, function\n" +
						"}"
					);
				endif;
				
				GetRuntimeIdentifierRef("InitializeAllDefaultPublicIdentifiers",procRef);
				if not procRef then
					CreateRuntimeProcedure("InitializeAllDefaultPublicIdentifiers",
						"block where warning_explicit_element_not_in_set:=off;\nAllDefaultPublicIdentifiers := {IndexIdentifiers | IdentifierType(IndexIdentifiers) in AllAllowedIdentifierTypes}\n - SectionIdentifiers('AIMMSWebUI')\n - SectionIdentifiers('AIMMSWebUIRuntime')\n - if 'AIMMSProLibrary' in AllSections then SectionIdentifiers('AIMMSProLibrary') else {} endif\n - if 'AimmsProGUI' in AllSections then SectionIdentifiers('AimmsProGUI') else {} endif;\nendblock;\n"
					, libRef,procRef);
				endif;
				
				me::Compile(libref);
				apply(procRef);
			}
			ElementParameter setRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Output;
			}
			ElementParameter libRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter procRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp';
			}
		}
		ExternalProcedure IdentifierHasBinaryRange_Internal {
			Arguments: (idName);
			DllName: CubeEngineLinkDLLPath;
			ReturnType: integer;
			Encoding: NativeUnicodeEncoding;
			BodyCall: {
				CE_IdentifierHasBinaryRange(
					scalar string : idName
				)
			}
			StringParameter idName {
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
	}

	Section Annotations_Support {
		Set Annotations {
			Index: a;
			Definition: data { 'webui::ElementTextIdentifier', 'webui::AnnotationsIdentifier', 'webui::TooltipIdentifier', 'webui::UponChangeProcedure', 'webui::FlagsIdentifier', 'webui::ElementHtmlIdentifier' };
		}
		StringParameter AnnotationValues {
			IndexDomain: IndexIdentifiers;
		}
		StringParameter AllAnnotationValues {
			IndexDomain: (a,IndexIdentifiers);
		}
		Procedure InitializeAnnotations {
			Body: {
				for (a) do
					GetAnnotationValues(a,AnnotationValues);
					AllAnnotationValues(a,IndexIdentifiers) := AnnotationValues(IndexIdentifiers);
				endfor;
			}
		}
		Procedure CheckForOldStyleAnnotations {
			Body: {
				OptionGetString("Check_for_old_style_WebUI_annotations",CheckForOldStyleWebUIAnnotations);
				if (CheckForOldStyleWebUIAnnotations = "No") then
					return;
				endif;
				
				OldStyleAnnotations := data { 'webui::AnnotationsIdentifier', 'webui::FlagsIdentifier', 'webui::TooltipIdentifier', 'webui::ItemTextIdentifier' };
				OldStyleAnnotationPostfix(oa) := data {
					'webui::AnnotationsIdentifier' : "annotations",
					'webui::FlagsIdentifier'       : "flags",
					'webui::TooltipIdentifier'     : "tooltips",
					'webui::ItemTextIdentifier'    : "text"
				};
				OldStyleAnnotationDescriptionSingular(oa) := data {
					'webui::AnnotationsIdentifier' : "annotation",
					'webui::FlagsIdentifier'       : "flag",
					'webui::TooltipIdentifier'     : "tooltip",
					'webui::ItemTextIdentifier'    : "text"
				};
				OldStyleAnnotationDescriptionPlural(oa) := data {
					'webui::AnnotationsIdentifier' : "annotations",
					'webui::FlagsIdentifier'       : "flags",
					'webui::TooltipIdentifier'     : "tooltips",
					'webui::ItemTextIdentifier'    : "text data"
				};
				
				for (oa) do
				
					empty AnnotationValues;
					GetAnnotationValues(oa,AnnotationValues);
					Postfix := OldStyleAnnotationPostfix(oa);
					Singular := OldStyleAnnotationDescriptionSingular(oa);
					Plural := OldStyleAnnotationDescriptionPlural(oa);
					InitialWarningPrinted := 0;
				
					for (IndexIdentifiers | not IsRuntimeIdentifier(IndexIdentifiers)) do
						AnnotationIdentifierName := FormatString("%e_%s",IndexIdentifiers,Postfix);
						if (SubString(IndexIdentifiers,0,15) = "webui_runtime::") then
							skip;
						endif;
						if (StringToElement(AllIdentifiers,AnnotationIdentifierName,0) <> '') then
							if (not AnnotationValues(IndexIdentifiers)) then
								if (not InitialWarningPrinted) then
									raise warning FormatString("Your model probably contains old-style WebUI %s. Please consider refactoring your model to use the '%e' model annotation to specify your %s.",Plural,oa,Plural);
									InitialWarningPrinted := 1;
								endif;
								raise warning FormatString("Identifier '%e' possesses the old-style WebUI %s '%s'",IndexIdentifiers,Singular,AnnotationIdentifierName);
							else
								AnnotationValueWithoutIndexDomain := AnnotationValues(IndexIdentifiers);
								PosOfFirstBracket := FindString(AnnotationValueWithoutIndexDomain,"(");
								if (PosOfFirstBracket > 0) then
									AnnotationValueWithoutIndexDomain := SubString(AnnotationValueWithoutIndexDomain,0,PosOfFirstBracket-1);
								endif;
								if (not FindString(
									SearchString  : AnnotationIdentifierName,
									Key           : AnnotationValueWithoutIndexDomain,
									CaseSensitive : 0, 
									IgnoreWhite   : 1)) then
									if (not InitialWarningPrinted) then
										raise warning FormatString("Your model probably contains unused old-style WebUI %s. Please consider refactoring your model to remove or rename them",Plural);
										InitialWarningPrinted := 1;
									endif;
									raise warning FormatString("Your model contains an old-style WebUI %s '%s' for identifier '%e' which differs from the %s as specified in the '%e' attribute (which is '%s'). Be aware that the old-style %s will be automatically used as a fallback once you clear the '%e' attribute. Please consider removing or renaming '%s' ",Singular,AnnotationIdentifierName,IndexIdentifiers,Singular,oa,AnnotationValues(IndexIdentifiers),Singular,oa,AnnotationIdentifierName);
								endif;
							endif;
						endif;
					endfor;
				
				endfor;
			}
			StringParameter AnnotationIdentifierName;
			Parameter InitialWarningPrinted {
				Default: 0;
			}
			Parameter PosOfFirstBracket;
			StringParameter AnnotationValueWithoutIndexDomain;
			Set OldStyleAnnotations {
				Index: oa;
			}
			StringParameter OldStyleAnnotationPostfix {
				IndexDomain: oa;
			}
			StringParameter OldStyleAnnotationDescriptionSingular {
				IndexDomain: oa;
			}
			StringParameter OldStyleAnnotationDescriptionPlural {
				IndexDomain: oa;
			}
			StringParameter Postfix;
			StringParameter Singular;
			StringParameter Plural;
			StringParameter CheckForOldStyleWebUIAnnotations;
		}
	}

		Section Public_User_Support_Declarations;
		Procedure CurrentUserBelongsToGroup {
			Arguments: (groupName);
			Body: {
				groupNameLowerCase := StringToLower(groupName);
				
				if ( pro::GetCurrentUserInfo(CurrentEnvironment,CurrentUserName,CurrentBucket,CurrentGroups,CurrentGroupName) ) then
					for (cg) do
						CurrentGroupNameLowerCase := StringToLower(CurrentGroupName(cg));
						if ( CurrentGroupNameLowerCase = groupNameLowerCase ) then
							return 1;
						endif;
					endfor;
				endif;
				
				return 0;
			}
			StringParameter groupName {
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter groupNameLowerCase;
			StringParameter CurrentEnvironment;
			StringParameter CurrentUserName;
			StringParameter CurrentBucket;
			Set CurrentGroups {
				Index: cg;
			}
			StringParameter CurrentGroupName {
				IndexDomain: cg;
			}
			StringParameter CurrentGroupNameLowerCase;
			Parameter ret;
		}
		Function GetIOFilePath {
			Arguments: (IOFileName);
			Range: string;
			Body: {
				if PROIsInitialized and not ProjectDeveloperMode then
					! The PRO case
					GetIOFilePath := pro::GetPROTempFolder + "/" + IOFileName;
				else
					! The non-PRO case
					GetIOFilePath := IOFileName;
				endif;
			}
			Comment: {
				"This function returns the complete path leading to an uploaded/downloaded file (including the name of the file itself!).
				It should be called from within procedures that are bound to the \'Upload\' or the \'Download\' widget.
				The result is different when running under PRO or when running in development mode. In the former case,
				the temporary PRO path is prepended to the file name. In the latter case, nothing is added."
			}
			StringParameter IOFileName {
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
	}
