		Section Public_Form_Support_Procedures {
			Procedure SetupForm {
				Arguments: (formId,selInMaster,detailsIdentifiers,validationHandler,updateEntryCallback);
				Body: {
					CheckCubeEngineLinkDLLPathHasBeenInitialized;
					
					GenerateAllPublicIdentifiersSet;
					
					AssertValidFormId(formId);
					
					GetOrCreateWebUIRuntimeLibrary(theWebUIRuntimeLibrary);
					
					CheckSetupFormArguments(selInMaster,detailsIdentifiers);
					
					SetupFormSection(formId, theFormSection);
					
					indexInSelectionInMaster := DomainIndex(selInMaster,1);
					rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster := IndexRange(indexInSelectionInMaster);
					
					theForm := StringToElement(RegisteredForms,formId,0);
					if ( theForm ) then
					
						!delete data change Monitor for MasterSelection identifiers
						if ( MasterSelectionChangeMonitorRef(theForm) ) then
							DataChangeMonitorUnregisterCallback(MasterSelectionChangeMonitorRef(theForm));
						endif;
					
						!delete data change Monitor for form data identifiers
						if ( FormDataChangeMonitorRef(theForm) ) then
							DataChangeMonitorUnregisterCallback(FormDataChangeMonitorRef(theForm));
						endif;
					
						! Remove any existing element
						RegisteredForms -= theForm;
						cleandependents RegisteredForms;
					endif;
					
					! Create new 'registered form' element
					SetElementAdd(RegisteredForms,theForm,formId);
					
					UpdateEntryCallbackProcedure(theForm) := updateEntryCallback;
					
					aRuntimeIdentifierRef := me::Create(FormatString("%e_currentSelection",theForm),'element parameter',theFormSection);
					me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'range', FormatString("%e",rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster));
					me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'definition',FormatString("first(%e|%e(%e))",indexInSelectionInMaster,selInMaster,indexInSelectionInMaster));
					
					aRuntimeIdentifierRef := me::Create(FormatString("%e_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing",theForm),'element parameter', theFormSection);
					me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'range', FormatString("%e",rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster));
					
					CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent(formId + "_IsFormDirty",'parameter',theFormSection);
					CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent(formId + "_IsNewEntry",'parameter',theFormSection);
					
					empty formIdentifiers;
					for (di) do
						! generate identifier of the form '<formName>_<di>'
					
						formIdentifierName := FormatString("%s_%e",formId,di);
						formIdentifierName := FindReplaceStrings(formIdentifierName,"::","_");
						formIdentifierType := IdentifierType(di);
					
						aRuntimeIdentifierRef := me::Create(formIdentifierName,formIdentifierType,theFormSection);
					
						switch (formIdentifierType) do
						'parameter' :
							if(IdentifierHasBinaryRange(di)) then
								me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'range',"binary");
							endif;
					
							! TODO copy default
							me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'unit',IdentifierUnit(di));
							me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'initial data',"0");
						'string parameter' :
							me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'default',"");
							me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'initial data',"\"\"");
						'element parameter' :
							me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'range',FormatString("%e",IdentifierElementRange(di)));
							me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'initial data',"\'\'");
						endswitch;
					
						/* Add the annotation string to the identifier itself */
						me::SetAnnotation(aRuntimeIdentifierRef, "webui::AnnotationsIdentifier", "webui_runtime::" + formIdentifierName + "_AnnotationString");
					
						formIdentifiers += aRuntimeIdentifierRef;
					
						aRuntimeIdentifierRef := me::Create(formIdentifierName + "_AnnotationString",'string parameter',theFormSection);
						me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'default',
							toJsonString(
								FormatString("[%s,%s]",
									toJsonString("nothing-selected-in-form"),
									CreateAnnotation("help-message", "no-"+selInMaster+"-selected")
								)
							)
						);
					
						aRuntimeIdentifierRef := me::Create(formIdentifierName + "_flags",'string parameter',theFormSection);
						me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'definition',
							FindReplaceStrings("\"readOnly\" onlyif (not (%f_IsNewEntry or %f_currentSelection))", "%f", formId)
						);
					endfor;
					
					procedureDataToFormBody += "webui::PreviousStringFormEntry('" + theForm + "',webui::fe) := webui::StringFormEntry('" + theForm + "',webui::fe);";
					
					CreateCopyFormToModeldentifiersProcedure(formId,detailsIdentifiers, aRuntimeIdentifierRef);
					CreateUpdateFormWithNewSelectionProcedure(formId, detailsIdentifiers, aRuntimeIdentifierRef);
					
					procedureCleanSelectionInMasterName := "CleanSelectionInMaster_" + formId;
					procedureCleanSelectionInMasterBody := "empty " + selInMaster + ";";
					
					AllFormFieldNames += detailsIdentifiers;
					
					CreateCallValidateFormProcedure(formId,detailsIdentifiers,validationHandler, callValidateFormProcedureRef);
					CreateFormOperationsProcedures(formId,selInMaster,detailsIdentifiers,rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster);
					
					CreateHandleMasterSelectionChangedProcedure(formId, selInMaster, detailsIdentifiers, HandleMasterSelectionChangedProcedureRef);
					monitoredIdentifiers := { selInMaster };
					webui::DataChangeMonitorRegisterCallback(monitoredIdentifiers, HandleMasterSelectionChangedProcedureRef, MasterSelectionChangeMonitorRef(theForm));
					
					! create data change monitor that monitors all form identifier that have been generated
					CreateHandleFormDataChangedProcedure(formId, detailsIdentifiers, callValidateFormProcedureRef, HandleDataChangedProcedureRef);
					webui::DataChangeMonitorRegisterCallback(formIdentifiers, HandleDataChangedProcedureRef, FormDataChangeMonitorRef(theForm));
					
					me::Compile(theWebUIRuntimeLibrary);
					
					AddToAllImplicitPublicIdentifiers(formId,detailsIdentifiers);
					
					webui::InitializeAnnotations;
				}
				Comment: {
					"
					formId: the name/id of the form, must be a valid AIMMS identifier name
					selInMaster: a one-dimensional parameter indexed over the same set that the detailsIndentifiers are indexed over
					detailsIdentifiers: a set of parameters indexed over the same set as the selInMaster
					validationHandler: a reference to a procedure(formData, validationErrors)
					where:
							formData(webui::ffn)          - A string parameter containing all the raw form values, indexed over the detailsIdentifiers
							validationErrors(webui::ffn)  - A string parameter, to be updated by this validationHandler, that should contain all
															validation errors encountered in formData; also indexed over the detailsIdentifiers.
															Use webui::CreateValidationError(\"validation-error-some-error-id\") to fill.
					updateEntryCallback: a reference to a procedure(formData,selectedElementName)
					where:
							formData(webui::ffn)          - A string parameter containing all the raw form values, indexed over the detailsIdentifiers
							selectedElementName           - The name of the element that this procedure should handle (i.e. create or update an element in the set
															which the selInMaster and the detailsIdentifiers are indexed over)."
				}
				ElementParameter callValidateFormProcedureRef {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Default: 'webui::NoOp';
				}
				ElementParameter theFormSection {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Default: 'webui::NoOp';
				}
				ElementParameter HandleMasterSelectionChangedProcedureRef {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Default: 'webui::NoOp';
				}
				ElementParameter HandleDataChangedProcedureRef {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Default: 'webui::NoOp';
				}
				Set monitoredIdentifiers {
					SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				Set formIdentifiers {
					SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				Parameter stat;
				StringParameter newEntryValue {
					IndexDomain: IndexIdentifiers;
				}
				ElementParameter selInMaster {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter indexInSelectionInMaster {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				ElementParameter rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				Set detailsIdentifiers {
					SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
					Index: di;
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter validationHandler {
					Range: AllProcedures;
					Default: 'webui::DefaultValidateFormProcedure';
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter updateEntryCallback {
					Range: AllProcedures;
					Default: 'webui::DefaultUpdateEntryCallback';
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter theForm {
					Range: RegisteredForms;
				}
				StringParameter formIdentifierName {
					Comment: "AllAttributeNames";
				}
				ElementParameter formIdentifierType {
					Range: AllIdentifierTypes;
				}
				StringParameter formIdentifierUnit;
				ElementParameter formAnnotationsIdentifier {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				ElementParameter aRuntimeIdentifierRef {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				StringParameter formAnnotationsIdentifierName {
					Comment: "AllAttributeNames";
				}
				StringParameter formAnnotationsIdentifierDefinition {
					Comment: "AllAttributeNames";
				}
				ElementParameter theFormDeclationSection {
					Range: AllSymbols;
				}
				ElementParameter theWebUIRuntimeLibrary {
					Range: AllSymbols;
				}
				StringParameter procedureCheckMasterSelectionHasChangedName;
				StringParameter procedureCheckMasterSelectionHasChangedBody;
				ElementParameter procedureSaveForm {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				StringParameter procedureSaveFormName;
				StringParameter procedureSaveFormBody;
				StringParameter procedureDataToFormName;
				StringParameter procedureDataToFormBody;
				StringParameter procedureFormToDataName;
				StringParameter procedureFormToDataBody;
				StringParameter procedureCleanSelectionInMasterName;
				StringParameter procedureCleanSelectionInMasterBody;
				StringParameter previousSelectionInMasterName;
				StringParameter previousSelectionInMasterRange;
				StringParameter formId {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Procedure DefaultValidateFormProcedure {
				Arguments: (formData,validationErrors);
				Body: {
					for(webui::ffn | (not ValidationErrors(webui::ffn)) and (IdentifierType(webui::ffn) = 'parameter')) do
						block
							parseResult := Val(formData(webui::ffn));
						onerror err do
							validationErrors(webui::ffn) := webui::CreateValidationError("validation-error-not-a-number");
							errh::MarkAsHandled(err);
						endblock;
					endfor;
				}
				StringParameter FormData {
					IndexDomain: (ffn);
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter validationErrors {
					IndexDomain: ffn;
					Property: InOut;
				}
				Parameter parseResult;
				ElementParameter err {
					Range: errh::PendingErrors;
					Default: 0;
				}
			}
			Function CreateValidationError {
				Arguments: (errorId,arg0,arg1,arg2,arg3,arg4);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					if(IsValidErrorId(errorId)) then
						validationErrorString := CreateAnnotation("validation-error", errorId, arg0, arg1, arg2, arg3, arg4);
						errorIdHash := CreateHash(validationErrorString);
						CreateValidationError := FormatString("%s|%n", validationErrorString, errorIdHash);
					else
						raise error "Not a valid errorId: " + errorId;
					endif;
				}
				Comment: {
					"
					errorId: a string starting with \"validation-error-\" and consisting of only A-Z a-z and hyphens/dashes/underscores.
					arg0...arg4: Scalar strings containing the model information that should go with the validation error.
								(This is used by the WebUI Runtime in the browser to produce i18n/internationalized messages.)"
				}
				StringParameter errorId {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter errorIdHash;
				StringParameter validationErrorString;
				StringParameter arg0 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg1 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg2 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg3 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg4 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
			}
			Function CreateAnnotation {
				Arguments: (annotationType,annotationId,arg0,arg1,arg2,arg3,arg4);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					! replace namespace separators by underscores
					normalizedAnnotationId := FindReplaceStrings(annotationId,"::","_");
					
					if(IsValidAnnotationId(normalizedAnnotationId)) then
						! Note: We always generate a JSON array with 5 strings, even if they are empty, because in AIMMS
						!       you cannot distinguish between undefined and ""
						CreateAnnotation := FormatString("{\"type\": \"%s\", \"id\": \"%s\", \"arguments\": [%s,%s,%s,%s,%s]}",
									annotationType,
									normalizedAnnotationId,
									toJsonString(arg0),
									toJsonString(arg1),
									toJsonString(arg2),
									toJsonString(arg3),
									toJsonString(arg4));
					else
						raise error "Not a valid annotationId: " + annotationId;
					endif;
				}
				Comment: {
					"
					errorId: a string starting with \"validation-error-\" and consisting of only A-Z a-z and hyphens/dashes."
				}
				StringParameter annotationType {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter annotationId {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter normalizedAnnotationId;
				StringParameter annotationJsonString;
				StringParameter arg0 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg1 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg2 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg3 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg4 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
			}
			Procedure AssertValidValidationError {
				Arguments: (str);
				Body: {
					isValid := 0;
					
					if(StringLength(str) = 0) then
						isValid := 1;
					endif;
					
					dividerPosition := FindString(str, "|");
					
					if(dividerPosition > 0) then
						errorId := SubString(str, 1, dividerPosition-1);
						errorHashString := SubString(str, dividerPosition+1, END_OF_STRING);
					
						isValid := errorHashString = CreateHash(errorId);
					endif;
					
					if(not isValid) then
						raise error "Not a valid validation error: " + str;
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter str {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter isValid;
				StringParameter errorId;
				StringParameter errorHashString;
				Parameter dividerPosition;
			}
			Function GetValidationError {
				Arguments: (validationError);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					! @TODO temporary solution so that we can pass validation-error-foo-bar (without the '|checksum' part ) to the runtime
					GetValidationError := SubString(validationError, 1, FindNthString(validationError, "|", -1)-1);
				}
				StringParameter validationError {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Procedure RequestPerformFormDialog {
				Arguments: (title,message,actions,onDone);
				Body: {
					FormDialogHandlerOnDone := OnDone;
					requestPerformWebUIDialog(title,message,actions,'webui::HandleFormDialogResult');
				}
				Set monitoredIdentifiers {
					SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				StringParameter actionsString;
				Set actions {
					Index: a;
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter title {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter message {
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter onDone {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Default: 'webui::NoOp1';
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
		}
		Procedure CheckCubeEngineLinkDLLPathHasBeenInitialized {
			Body: {
				if ( CubeEngineLinkDLLPath = "" ) then
					raise error "webui::SetupForm error: 'SetupForm' can only be called after the AimmsWebUI library has been initialized.";
				endif;
			}
		}
		Procedure CheckSetupFormArguments {
			Arguments: (selInMaster,detailsIdentifiers);
			Body: {
				if ( ( IdentifierType(selInMaster) <> 'parameter' ) or ( IdentifierDimension(selInMaster) <> 1 ) ) then
					raise error "webui::SetupForm error: The 2nd argument of 'SetupForm' should be a 1-dimensional numerical identifier";
				endif;
				
				indexInSelectionInMaster := DomainIndex(selInMaster,1);
				rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster := IndexRange(indexInSelectionInMaster);
				
				for (di) do
					if ( ( IdentifierType(di) <> 'parameter' ) and ( IdentifierType(di) <> 'string parameter' ) and ( IdentifierType(di) <> 'element parameter' )) then
						raise error "webui::SetupForm error: The procedure only supports 'numerical', 'string-valued' and 'element-valued' form field identifiers";
					endif;
					if ( IdentifierDimension(di) <> 1 ) then
						raise error "webui::SetupForm error: All form field identifiers should be 1-dimensional";
					endif;
					indexInSelectionInDetailIdentifier := DomainIndex(di,1);
					rangeOfIndexInSelectionInDetailIdentifier := IndexRange(indexInSelectionInDetailIdentifier);
					if ( rangeOfIndexInSelectionInDetailIdentifier <> rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster ) then
						raise error "webui::SetupForm error: All form field identifiers should have the same index domain as the identifier that has been specified as the 2nd argument";
					endif;
				endfor;
			}
			Set detailsIdentifiers {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				Index: di;
			}
			ElementParameter selInMaster {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter indexInSelectionInMaster {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter indexInSelectionInDetailIdentifier {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter rangeOfIndexInSelectionInDetailIdentifier {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
		Procedure CreateCallValidateFormProcedure {
			Arguments: (formId,fields,validateFormProcedure,callValidateFormProcedureRef);
			Body: {
				GetFormSection(formId, theFormSection);
				
				validateFormProcedureName := FormatString("%e", validateFormProcedure);
				anIdentifierRef := me::Create(formId+"_formData", 'string parameter', theFormSection, 0);
				me::SetAttribute(anIdentifierRef, 'index domain', "(webui::ffn)");
				anIdentifierRef := me::Create(formId+"_validationErrors", 'string parameter', theFormSection, 0);
				me::SetAttribute(anIdentifierRef, 'index domain', "(webui::ffn)");
				
				callValidateFormProcedureRef := me::Create(formId + "_CallValidateForm", 'procedure', theFormSection, 0);
				me::SetAttribute(callValidateFormProcedureRef, 'body',
					! copy scalar identifiers to formData
					ExpandTemplate(fields, formId+"_formData('%e') := "+formId+"_%i;\n") +
				
					! validateFormProcedureName(formData, validationErrors)
					validateFormProcedureName+"("+formId+"_formData, "+formId+"_validationErrors);\n" +
				
					! check if the validation errors are either empty (no validation error) or valid
					ExpandTemplate(fields, "webui::AssertValidValidationError("+formId+"_validationErrors('%e'));\n") +
				
					! perform built-in validation error checking
					"webui::DefaultValidateFormProcedure("+formId+"_formData, "+formId+"_validationErrors);\n" +
				
					! copy the validation errors back to scalar identifiers annotations
					ExpandTemplate(fields, formId+"_%i_AnnotationString := if "+formId+"_%i then FormatString(\"[%s]\", webui::GetValidationError("+formId+"_validationErrors('%e'))) else FormatString(\"[%s]\", webui::CreateAnnotation(\"help-message\", \"form-enter-%i\")) endif;\n")
				);
				
				isValidFormParameterRef := me::Create(formId + "_IsFormValid", 'parameter', theFormSection, 0);
				me::SetAttribute(isValidFormParameterRef, 'definition',
					"if ( exists[ webui::ffn | webui_runtime::"+formId+ "_validationErrors(webui::ffn) ] ) then 0 else 1 endif"
				);
			}
			ElementParameter theFormSection {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp';
			}
			StringParameter validateFormProcedureName;
			ElementParameter anIdentifierRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			Set fields {
				Index: f;
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter formId {
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter validateFormProcedure {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter callValidateFormProcedureRef {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Property: Output;
			}
			ElementParameter isValidFormParameterRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
		Procedure CreateUpdateFormWithNewSelectionProcedure {
			Arguments: (formId,fields,procedureRef);
			Body: {
				GetFormSection(formId, theFormSection);
				
				procedureRef := me::Create(formId + "_UpdateFormWithNewSelection", 'procedure', theFormSection, 0);
				me::SetAttribute(procedureRef, 'body',
					FindReplaceStrings(
						"%f_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing := %f_currentSelection;\n"+
				
						ExpandTemplate(fields, "empty %f_%i;\n")+
						ExpandTemplate(fields, "empty %f_%i_AnnotationString;\n") +
				
						"if ( %f_IsNewEntry ) then\n"+
							ExpandTemplate(fields, "    %f_%i_AnnotationString := webui::CreateAnnotation(\"help-message\", \"form-enter-%i\");\n")+
						"elseif ( %f_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing ) then\n"+
							ExpandTemplate(fields, "    %f_%i_AnnotationString := \"\";\n") +
							ExpandTemplate(fields, "    %f_%i := %e(%f_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing);\n")+
						"endif;\n"+
				
						"webui::DataChangeMonitorReset(webui::FormDataChangeMonitorRef('"+StringToElement(RegisteredForms, formId)+"'));\n"+
						"%f_IsFormDirty := 0;\n",
						"%f",
						formId
					)
				);
			}
			ElementParameter theFormSection {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp';
			}
			Set fields {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				Index: f;
				Property: Input;
			}
			Set numericalFields {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				Index: nf;
			}
			Set stringFields {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				Index: sf;
			}
			StringParameter formId {
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter procedureRef {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Property: Output;
			}
		}
		Procedure CreateCopyFormToModeldentifiersProcedure {
			Arguments: (formId,fields,procedureRef);
			Body: {
				GetFormSection(formId, theFormSection);
				
				CreateRuntimeProcedure(
					formId + "_CopyFormToModelIdentifiers",
					"if ( " + formId + "_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing ) then\n" +
						! copy scalar identifiers to the model data
						ExpandTemplate(fields, "    %e(" + formId + "_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing) := "+formId+"_%i;\n") +
						"endif;",
					theFormSection,
					procedureRef
				);
			}
			ElementParameter theFormSection {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp';
			}
			Set fields {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				Index: f;
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter formId {
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter procedureRef {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Property: Output;
			}
		}
		Procedure CreateHandleMasterSelectionChangedProcedure {
			Arguments: (formId, selInMaster, fields,procedureRef);
			Body: {
				GetFormSection(formId, theFormSection);
				GetOrCreateWebUIRuntimeLibrary(theWebUIRuntimeLibrary);
				
				CreateRuntimeProcedure(
					formId + "_HandleDialogResultOnMasterSelectionChangedWhileStateIsDirty",
					FindReplaceStrings(
					"update %f_currentSelection;\n" +
					"if ( webui::FormDialogHandlerArg = \"apply-and-continue\" ) then\n" +
					"\t%f_SaveForm;\n" +
					"\t%f_UpdateFormWithNewSelection;\n" +
					"elseif ( webui::FormDialogHandlerArg = \"discard-and-continue\" ) then\n" +
					"\t%f_IsNewEntry := 0;\n" +
					"\twebui::FormIsNewEntry('%f') := 0;\n" +
					"\t%f_UpdateFormWithNewSelection;\n" +
					"elseif ( webui::FormDialogHandlerArg = \"cancel\" ) then\n" +
					"\t" + selInMaster + "(%f_currentSelection) := 0;\n" +
					"\t" + selInMaster + "(%f_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing) := 1;\n" +
					"\twebui::DataChangeMonitorReset(webui::MasterSelectionChangeMonitorRef('%f'));\n"+
					"else\n" +
					"\traise error \"Unsupported action\";\n" +
					"endif;"
					, "%f", formId),
					theFormSection,
					procedureRef
				);
				
				CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent("dialogActions", 'set', theWebUIRuntimeLibrary);
				CreateRuntimeProcedure(
					formId + "_HandleMasterSelectionChanged",
					FindReplaceStrings(
					"update %f_currentSelection;\n" +
					"if %f_IsFormDirty then\n" +
					"\tif (%f_currentSelection <> %f_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing) then\n" +
					"\t\tdialogActions := {'apply-and-continue','discard-and-continue','cancel'};\n" +
					"\t\tif ( not %f_IsFormValid ) then\n" +
					"\t\t\tdialogActions -= 'apply-and-continue';\n" +
					"\t\tendif;\n" +
					"\t\twebui::requestPerformFormDialog(\"form-dirty-title\", \"form-dirty-message\", dialogActions, 'webui_runtime::%f_HandleDialogResultOnMasterSelectionChangedWhileStateIsDirty');\n" +
					"\tendif;" +
					"else\n" +
					"\t%f_IsNewEntry := 0;\n" +
					"\twebui::FormIsNewEntry('%f') := 0;\n" +
					"\t%f_UpdateFormWithNewSelection;\n" +
					"endif;", "%f", formId),
					theFormSection,
					procedureRef
				);
			}
			ElementParameter selInMaster {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter theWebUIRuntimeLibrary {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter theFormSection {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp';
			}
			Set fields {
				Index: f;
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter formId {
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter procedureRef {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Property: Output;
			}
		}
		Procedure CreateFormOperationsProcedures {
			Arguments: (formId,selInMaster,fields,rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster);
			Body: {
				GetFormSection(formId, theFormSection);
				GetOrCreateWebUIRuntimeLibrary(theWebUIRuntimeLibrary);
				
				CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent("dialogActions", 'set', theWebUIRuntimeLibrary);
				CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent("NewEntryName", 'string parameter', theWebUIRuntimeLibrary);
				
				CreateRuntimeProcedure(
					formId + "_SaveForm",
					FindReplaceStrings(
						"if (%f_IsFormDirty) then\n" +
						"\tif (not %f_IsFormValid) then\n"+
						"\tdialogActions := {'ok'};\n"+
						"\t! Note: requestPerformWebUIDialog instead of requestPerformFormDialog since there is no onDone\n"+
						"\twebui::requestPerformWebUIDialog(\"form-not-valid-title\", \"form-not-valid-message\", dialogActions, '');\n"+
						"\telse\n"+
				
						"\tif ( not webui_runtime::"+ formId +"_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing ) then\n"+
									! copy scalar identifiers to formData
									ExpandTemplate(fields, "\t\t\t%f_formData('%e') := "+formId+"_%i;\n") +
						"\t\tendif ;\n"+
						"\tApply(webui::UpdateEntryCallbackProcedure('%f'), %f_formData, NewEntryName);\n"+
						"\t%f_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing := StringToElement("+rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster+",NewEntryName);\n" +
						"\t%f_CopyFormToModelIdentifiers;\n" +
				
						"\t%f_IsFormDirty := 0;\n" +
						"\t%f_IsNewEntry := 0;\n"+
						"\twebui::FormIsNewEntry('%f') := 0;\n"+
						"\t" + selInMaster + "(%f_theSelectedItemThatTheFormIsShowing) := 1;\n" +
						"\tendif;\n" +
						"endif;\n",
						"%f",
						formId
					),
					theFormSection, procedureRef );
				
				! New Entry When Dirty Dialog Handler
				CreateRuntimeProcedure(
					formId + "_HandleFormDialogResultOnNewEntryWhileStateIsDirty",
					FindReplaceStrings(
						"if ( webui::FormDialogHandlerArg = \"apply-and-continue\" ) then\n" +
						"\t%f_SaveForm;\n" +
						"\t%f_IsNewEntry := 1;\n"+
						"\twebui::FormIsNewEntry('%f') := 1;\n"+
						"\t" + selInMaster + "(%f_currentSelection) := 0;\n" +
						"\twebui::DataChangeMonitorReset(webui::MasterSelectionChangeMonitorRef('%f'));\n" +
						"\t%f_UpdateFormWithNewSelection;\n" +
						"elseif ( webui::FormDialogHandlerArg = \"discard-and-continue\" ) then\n" +
						"\t%f_IsNewEntry := 1;\n" +
						"\twebui::FormIsNewEntry('%f') := 1;\n"+
						"\t" + selInMaster + "(%f_currentSelection) := 0;\n" +
						"\twebui::DataChangeMonitorReset(webui::MasterSelectionChangeMonitorRef('%f'));\n" +
						"\t%f_UpdateFormWithNewSelection;\n" +
						"elseif ( webui::FormDialogHandlerArg = \"cancel\" ) then\n" +
						"\t! do nothing\n" +
						"else\n" +
						"\traise error \"Unsupported action\";\n" +
						"endif;\n",
						"%f",
						formId
					),
					theFormSection, procedureRef );
				
				CreateRuntimeProcedure(
					formId + "_NewEntry",
					FindReplaceStrings(
						"if ( %f_IsFormDirty ) then\n" +
						"\tdialogActions := {'apply-and-continue','discard-and-continue','cancel'};\n" +
						"\tif ( not %f_IsFormValid) then\n" +
						"\tdialogActions -= 'apply-and-continue';\n" +
						"\tendif;\n" +
						"\twebui::requestPerformFormDialog(\"form-dirty-title\", \"form-dirty-message\", dialogActions, 'webui_runtime::%f_HandleFormDialogResultOnNewEntryWhileStateIsDirty');\n" +
						"else\n" +
						"\t%f_IsNewEntry := 1;\n" +
						"\twebui::FormIsNewEntry('%f') := 1;\n" +
						"\t" + selInMaster + "(%f_currentSelection) := 0;\n" +
						"\twebui::DataChangeMonitorReset(webui::MasterSelectionChangeMonitorRef('%f'));\n" +
						"\t%f_UpdateFormWithNewSelection;\n" +
						"endif;\n",
						"%f",
						formId
					),
					theFormSection, procedureRef );
				
				CreateRuntimeProcedure(
					formId + "_HandleDialogresultOnDeleteEntry",
					FindReplaceStrings(
						"if ( webui::FormDialogHandlerArg = \"yes\" ) then\n" +
						ExpandTemplate(fields,"    empty %e(%f_currentSelection);\n") +
						"    " + rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster + " -= %f_currentSelection;\n" +
						"    cleanup "  + selInMaster + ";\n" +
						"    %f_IsNewEntry := 0;\n" +
						"    webui::FormIsNewEntry('%f') := 0;\n" +
						"    %f_UpdateFormWithNewSelection;\n" +
						"elseif ( webui::FormDialogHandlerArg = \"no\" ) then\n" +
						"    ! do nothing\n" +
						"else\n" +
						"    raise error \"Unsupported action\";\n" +
						"endif;",
						"%f",
						formId
					),
					theFormSection, procedureRef );
				
				CreateRuntimeProcedure(
					formId + "_DeleteEntry",
					FindReplaceStrings(
						"if ( %f_currentSelection ) then\n" +
						"\tdialogActions := {'yes','no'};\n" +
						"\twebui::requestPerformFormDialog(\"delete-confirmation-title\", \"delete-confirmation-message\", dialogActions, 'webui_runtime::%f_HandleDialogResultOnDeleteEntry');\n" +
						"endif;\n",
						"%f",
						formId
					),
					theFormSection, procedureRef );
			}
			ElementParameter selInMaster {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter theWebUIRuntimeLibrary {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter theFormSection {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp';
			}
			Set fields {
				Index: f;
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter formId {
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter procedureRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter rangeOfIndexInSelectionInMaster {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Input;
			}
		}

		Procedure AssertValidFormId {
			Arguments: (formId);
			Body: {
				! @TODO  Add a regex matcher to AIMMS!
				
				isValid := 0;
				
				strLength := StringLength(formId);
				
				for (i in {1 .. strLength}) do
					charNum := CharacterNumber(charAt(formId, i));
				
					if((charNum >= CharacterNumber("A") and charNum <= CharacterNumber("Z")) or
					   (charNum >= CharacterNumber("a") and charNum <= CharacterNumber("z")) or
					   (i > 1 and (charNum >= CharacterNumber("0") and charNum <= CharacterNumber("9"))) or
					    charNum = CharacterNumber("_")) then
						isValid := 1;
					else
						isValid := 0;
						break;
					endif;
				endfor;
				
				if(not isValid) then
					raise error "Invalid form id: " + formId;
				else
					return isValid;
				endif;
			}
			StringParameter formId {
				Property: Input;
			}
			Parameter isValid;
			Parameter strLength;
			Index i {
				Range: Integers;
			}
			Parameter charNum;
		}
		Procedure IsValidAnnotationId {
			Arguments: (annotationId);
			Body: {
				! @TODO  Add a regex matcher to AIMMS!
				
				isValid := 0;
				
				strLength := StringLength(annotationId);
				
				for (i in {1 .. strLength}) do
					charNum := CharacterNumber(charAt(annotationId, i));
				
					if((charNum >= CharacterNumber("A") and charNum <= CharacterNumber("Z")) or
					   (charNum >= CharacterNumber("a") and charNum <= CharacterNumber("z")) or
					   (charNum >= CharacterNumber("0") and charNum <= CharacterNumber("9") and i > 1) or
					    charNum = CharacterNumber("-") or  charNum = CharacterNumber("_")) then
						isValid := 1;
					else
						isValid := 0;
						break;
					endif;
				endfor;
				
				return isValid;
			}
			StringParameter annotationId {
				Property: Input;
			}
			Parameter isValid;
			Parameter strLength;
			Index i {
				Range: Integers;
			}
			Parameter charNum;
		}
		Procedure IsValidErrorId {
			Arguments: (errorId);
			Body: {
				return IsValidAnnotationId(errorId) and StartsWith(errorId, "validation-error-");
			}
			StringParameter errorId {
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
		Procedure CreateHash {
			Arguments: (aString);
			Body: {
				strLength := StringLength(aString);
				
				hash := 0;
				
				for (i in {1 .. strLength}) do
					hash += CharacterNumber(charAt(aString, i));
				endfor;
				
				return hash;
			}
			StringParameter aString {
				Property: Input;
			}
			Parameter hash;
			Parameter strLength;
			Index i {
				Range: Integers;
			}
		}
		Procedure NoOp1Form {
			Arguments: (form);
			Body: {
				!Does nothing
			}
			ElementParameter form {
				Range: RegisteredForms;
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
	}

		Procedure CaseFileSave {
			Arguments: (url,selectedContentType);
			Body: {
				ret := Apply(ActualCaseFileSave,url,selectedContentType);
				
				if (PROIsInitialized) then
					pro::storage::ExistsObject(url, objectExists);
					if objectExists then
						CreateCreatorFile(url); ! @TODO What if this fails?
					endif;
				endif;
				
				if not ret then
				    raise warning "There were errors during the case save, please check the log files for details.";
				endif;
				
				return ret;
			}
			StringParameter url {
				Property: Input;
			}

				Index: ict;
				Property: Input;

		Section Util {
			Procedure FileExistsOnProStorage {
				Arguments: (url);
				Body: {
					targetFolder := GetTargetFolder(url);
					targetFile := GetTargetFile(url);
					
					! strip "pro:" from path
					len := StringLength(targetFolder);
					targetFolder := SubString(targetFolder,5,len-1);
					
					pro::storage::GetObjectInfo(targetFolder,targetFile,objFileName,objFileType,numberOfVersions,authorization,lastVersionID);
					
					return numberOfVersions > 0;
				}
				StringParameter objFileType;
				StringParameter objFileName;
				StringParameter lastVersionID;
				StringParameter authorization;
				Parameter numberOfVersions;
				StringParameter targetFile;
				StringParameter targetFolder;
				StringParameter url {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter len;
			}
			Function GetTargetFolder {
				Arguments: (url);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					GetTargetFolder := SubString(url, 1, FindNthString(url, "/", -1));
				}
				StringParameter url {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function GetTargetFile {
				Arguments: (url);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					GetTargetFile := SubString(url, FindNthString(url, "/", -1)+1, END_OF_STRING);
				}
				StringParameter url {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Parameter END_OF_STRING {
				Definition: 999999;
			}
			Set AccessFlags {
				Index: ac;
				Definition: data { x, w, r };
			}
			Procedure CheckFlag {
				Arguments: (value,flag);
				Body: {
					return Mod(value, 2^Ord(flag)) >= 2^(Ord(flag)-1);
				}
				Parameter value {
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter flag {
					Range: AccessFlags;
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
		}
		Procedure CreateCreatorFile {
			Arguments: (url);
			Body: {
				if ( PROIsInitialized ) then
				
					targetFolder := GetTargetFolder(url);
					targetFile := GetTargetFile(url);
				
					! strip "pro:" from path
					len := StringLength(targetFolder);
					targetFolder := SubString(targetFolder,5,len-1);
				
					ret := pro::storage::GetObjectInfo(targetFolder,targetFile,objFileName,objFileType,numberOfVersions,authorization,lastVersionID);
				
					ownerFilePath := ToCreatorUrl(url);
				
					if not FileExists( "./creator.template" ) then
						write EmptySet to file "./creator.template";
					endif;
				
					pro::SaveFileToCentralStorage("./creator.template", ownerFilePath, authorization);
				
				endif;
			}
			Parameter ret;
			Parameter len;
			Set EmptySet {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			StringParameter ownerFilePath;
			StringParameter targetFile;
			StringParameter targetFolder;
			StringParameter objFileType;
			StringParameter objFileName;
			StringParameter lastVersionID;
			StringParameter authorization;
			Parameter numberOfVersions;
			StringParameter url {
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
		Function ToCreatorUrl {
			Arguments: (url);
			Range: string;
			Body: {
				targetFolder := GetTargetFolder(url);
				targetFile := GetTargetFile(url);
				
				ToCreatorUrl := FormatString( "%s%s%s.creator.%s@%s", targetFolder, if targetFolder <> "" then "/" else "" endif, targetFile, pro::PROUserName, pro::PROEnvironment);
			}
			StringParameter targetFile;
			StringParameter targetFolder;
			StringParameter url {
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
		Function CreatorFilePresentInObjectSet {
			Arguments: (caseName,ObjectSet,ObjectFileName);
			Body: {
				CreatorFilePresentInObjectSet := 0;
				
				creatorFileNameStart := FormatString("%s.creator.", caseName);
				
				for (os | ObjectFileName(os) = "creator.template") do
					fName := FormatString("%e",os);
					if ( webui::StartsWith(fName, creatorFileNameStart)) then
						CreatorFilePresentInObjectSet := 1;
						return;
					endif;
				endfor;
			}
			StringParameter caseName {
				Property: Input;
			}
			Set ObjectSet {
				Index: os;
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter ObjectFileName {
				IndexDomain: os;
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter creatorFileNameStart;
			StringParameter fName;
		}
		Procedure ListObjectsWithEffectiveAuthorization {
			Arguments: (path,ObjectSet,ObjectFileName,ObjectType,ObjectSize,EffectiveAuth,MaxKeys,OffSet,separator);
			Body: {
				pro::storage::ListObjects(path,ObjectSet,ObjectFileName,ObjectType,ObjectSize,BucketAuth,MaxKeys,OffSet,separator);
				LocalLogInfo("pro::storage::ListObjects (when searching for pro case files): " + SetToJsonList(ObjectSet));
				EffectiveAuth(os) := 0;
				for (os) do
					caseName := FormatString("%e",os);
					len := StringLength(caseName);
					if ( SubString(caseName,len-4,len) = ".data" ) then
						! It's a case file (and not a creator file)
						EffectiveAuth(os) := 4;
						if (webui::StartsWith(path, "/UserData")) then
							! User case, owner always has read and write access
							EffectiveAuth(os) := 6;
						elseif (CreatorFilePresentInObjectSet(caseName, ObjectSet, ObjectFileName)) then
							! Shared case, check ObjectSet for creator file
							EffectiveAuth(os) := 6;
						else 
							! Fallback, ask PRO to check for createor file
							caseUrl := FormatString("pro:/%s/%e",path,os);
							creatorFileName := webui::proint::ToCreatorUrl(caseUrl);
							if (webui::proint::FileExistsOnProStorage(creatorFileName)) then
								EffectiveAuth(os) := 6;
							endif;
						endif;
					endif;
				endfor;
			}
			StringParameter creatorFileName;
			Set ObjectSet {
				Index: os;
				Parameter: aObject;
				Property: Output;
			}
			StringParameter path {
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter ObjectFileName {
				IndexDomain: os;
				Property: Output;
			}
			StringParameter ObjectType {
				IndexDomain: os;
				Property: Output;
			}
			Parameter ObjectSize {
				IndexDomain: os;
				Property: Output;
			}
			Parameter EffectiveAuth {
				IndexDomain: os;
				Property: Output;
			}
			Parameter MaxKeys {
				Default: -1;
				Property: Optional;
			}
			Parameter OffSet {
				Default: 0;
				Property: Optional;
			}
			StringParameter separator {
				Default: "/";
				Property: Optional;
			}
			StringParameter BucketAuth {
				IndexDomain: os;
			}
			StringParameter caseName;
			StringParameter caseUrl;
			Parameter len;
		}
		Procedure HasReadAccess {
			Arguments: (authMask);
			Body: {
				return CheckFlag(authMask, 'r');
			}
			Parameter authMask {
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
		Procedure HasWriteAccess {
			Arguments: (authMask);
			Body: {
				return CheckFlag(authMask, 'w');
			}
			Parameter authMask {
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
	}
	Section Data_Management {
		Section Public_Data_Management_Declarations {
			DeclarationSection WebUI_State_Hook_Declarations {
				Comment: {
					"By default a WebUI application will save its state upon AIMMS session termination and restore that state upon
					starting the WebUI application. The WebUI application does so by saving/restoring all identifiers into/from a
					case in PRO storage. If you want to override this behavior, e.g. because you want to save less or nothing at all,
					you can override the default values of the following identifiers in e.g. MainInitialization:
					
					webui::ExistsWebUIStateHook := \'MyExistsState\';
					webui::RestoreWebUIStateHook := \'MyRestoreState\';
					webui::SaveWebUIStateHook := \'MySaveState\';
					
					These override procedures should have no arguments, and return nothing, except for the \'MyExistsState\' procedure,
					which should return 1 if there is a state file available or 0 when there is not. You can implement your own
					overrides any way you see fit. It is however inadvisable to interact with the user (e.g. by dialogs) since these
					procedures are ran during initialization and termination, typically moments in which not all webui-functionality
					might be available."
				}
				ElementParameter ExistsWebUIStateHook {
					Range: AllProcedures;
					Default: 'webui::DefaultExistsWebUIState';
					Comment: {
						"Should return 0 if no state is present, 1 otherwise.
						See Comment attribute of \'WebUI State Hook Declarations\' section for more info."
					}
				}
				ElementParameter RestoreWebUIStateHook {
					Range: AllProcedures;
					Default: 'webui::DefaultRestoreWebUIState';
					Comment: "See Comment attribute of \'WebUI State Hook Declarations\' section for more info.";
				}
				ElementParameter SaveWebUIStateHook {
					Range: AllProcedures;
					Default: 'webui::DefaultSaveWebUIState';
					Comment: "See Comment attribute of \'WebUI State Hook Declarations\' section for more info.";
				}
			}
			Section Interface_Procedures {
				Procedure SaveAndReplaceActiveCase {
					Arguments: (casename,statusCode,statusDescription);
					Body: {
						if (CaseReadOnly(CurrentCase)) then
							statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('UNAUTHORIZED');
							statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Could not save to read-only case '%s'.", statusCode, casename);
						else
							if (SaveCase(CurrentCasePath, statusCode, statusDescription)) then
								LoadAndSetActiveCase(casename,statusCode,statusDescription);
								if (statusCode = ReturnStatusCode('OK')) then
									statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Successfully saved old active case and set case '%s' as active.", statusCode, casename);
								endif;
							endif;
						
						endif;
					}
					StringParameter casename {
						Property: Input;
					}
					Parameter statusCode {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter statusDescription {
						Property: Output;
					}
				}
				Procedure RefreshAllCasesEx {
					Arguments: (statusCode,statusDescription);
					Body: {
						RefreshAllCases;
						statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('OK');
						statusDescription := FormatString("%i: OK.", statusCode);
					}
					Parameter statusCode {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter statusDescription {
						Property: Output;
					}
				}
				Procedure LoadAndSetActiveCase {
					Arguments: (casename,statusCode,statusDescription);
					Body: {
						success := LoadCase(casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
						
						if (success) then
							SetActiveCase(casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
							CurrentCaseIsDirty := 0;
						
							if (statusCode = ReturnStatusCode('OK')) then
								statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Successfully loaded and set as active case '%s'.", statusCode, casename);
							endif;
						endif;
					}
					Parameter success;
					StringParameter casename {
						Property: Input;
					}
					Parameter statusCode {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter statusDescription {
						Property: Output;
					}
				}
				Procedure SetActiveCase {
					Arguments: (casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
					Body: {
						empty success;
						
						caseUrl := GetCaseUrl(casename);
						
						if ( CaseFileSetCurrent(caseUrl) ) then
							CurrentCasePath := CasePath(CurrentCase);
							bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"pro:/");
							if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then
								! PRO Storage case
								requestDescription := bareCasename;
							else
								bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"file:/");
								if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then
									! Case in project folder
									requestDescription := bareCasename;
								else
									requestDescription := casename;
								endif;
							endif;
							success := 1;
							statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('OK');
							statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Set case '%s' as active.", statusCode, casename);
						else
							requestDescription := "";
							CurrentCasePath := "";
							success := 0;
							statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('ERROR');
							statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Could not set case '%s' as active.", statusCode, casename);
						endif;
						
						pro::SetDefaultRequestDescription(requestDescription);
						
						return success;
					}
					StringParameter casename {
						Property: Input;
					}
					Parameter statusCode {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter statusDescription {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter caseUrl;
					StringParameter bareCasename;
					StringParameter requestDescription;
					Parameter success;
				}
				Procedure LoadCase {
					Arguments: (casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
					Body: {
						empty success, caseUrl;
						
						caseUrl := GetCaseUrl(casename);
						
						if ( StringLength(caseUrl) > 0 ) then
							LocalLogInfo("Loading WebUI case from : " + caseUrl);
						
							block
								if ( proint::CaseFileLoad(caseUrl) ) then
									success := 1;
									statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('OK');
									statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' successfully loaded.", statusCode, casename);
								else
									success := 0;
									statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('BAD_REQUEST');
									statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Unable to load case '%s'!", statusCode, casename);
								endif;
							onerror err do
								if ( FindString(errh::Message(err),"-case-") and FindString(errh::Message(err),"is not an integer.") ) then
									errh::MarkAsHandled(err);
									success := 1;
									statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('OK');
									statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' successfully loaded.", statusCode, casename);
								else
									success := 0;
									statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('ERROR');
									statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Unable to load case '%s'!", statusCode, casename);
								endif;
							endblock;
						endif;
						
						return success;
					}
					Parameter statusCode {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter statusDescription {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter casename {
						Property: Input;
					}
					StringParameter bareCasename;
					StringParameter storageServerPath;
					StringParameter caseUrl;
					ElementParameter err {
						Range: errh::PendingErrors;
					}
					Parameter success;
				}
				Procedure SaveCase {
					Arguments: (casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
					Body: {
						empty success, caseUrl;
						
						if ( IsLocalCase(casename) ) then
							! Case in project folder, create Data folder if not yet present
							if ( not DirectoryExists(LocalCaseDirectory) ) then
								DirectoryCreate(LocalCaseDirectory);
							endif;
						endif;
						
						caseUrl := GetCaseUrl(casename);
						
						if ( StringLength(caseUrl) > 0 ) then
							LocalLogInfo("Saving WebUI case to : " + caseUrl);
							if ( proint::CaseFileSave(caseUrl,CaseFileContentType) ) then
								success := 1;
							endif;
						endif;
						
						if ( success ) then
							statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('CREATED');
							statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' successfully saved.", statusCode, casename);
							CurrentCaseIsDirty := 0;
							RefreshAllCases;
						else
							statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('BAD_REQUEST');
							statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' could not be saved!", statusCode, casename);
						endif;
						
						return success;
					}
					Parameter statusCode {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter statusDescription {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter casename {
						Property: Input;
					}
					StringParameter bareCasename;
					StringParameter storageServerPath;
					StringParameter caseUrl;
					Parameter success;
				}
				Procedure CreateCase {
					Arguments: (casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
					Body: {
						numberOfversions := ExistsCase(casename);
						if ( numberOfversions > 0 ) then
							statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('CONFLICT');
							statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Could not create case '%s'. Case already exists!", statusCode, casename);
							return 0;
						else
							success := SaveCase(casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
						
							if ( success ) then
								success := SetActiveCase(casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
						
								if (success) then
									statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('CREATED');
									statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' successfully created and set as active.", statusCode, casename);
								endif;
							else
								statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('BAD_REQUEST');
								statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' could not be created!", statusCode, casename);
							endif;
						
							return success;
						endif;
					}
					Parameter success;
					StringParameter casename {
						Property: Input;
					}
					Parameter numberOfVersions;
					Parameter statusCode {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter statusDescription {
						Property: Output;
					}
				}
				Procedure DeleteCase {
					Arguments: (casename, statusCode, statusDescription);
					Body: {
						empty success;
						
						if ( ExistsCase(casename) ) then
						
							bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"pro:/");
							if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then
								! PRO Storage case: on the PRO storage server, two files, 'case.data' and 'case.data.types' are associated with the 'case' case.
								dataTypesCasename := FormatString("%s.data.types",bareCasename);
								bareCaseName := FormatString("%s.data",bareCasename);
								creatorFileName := proint::ToCreatorUrl(bareCaseName);
								display "CREATORFILENAME", creatorFileName;
								storageType := GetStorageType(casename);
								storageServerPath := GetCaseStorageServerPath(storageType) + "/";
								! strip "pro:" from path
								len := StringLength(storageServerPath);
								storageServerPath := SubString(storageServerPath,5,len-1);
								if ( PROIsInitialized ) then
									pro::storage::DeleteObjectAllVersions(storageServerPath,bareCasename);
									pro::storage::DeleteObjectAllVersions(storageServerPath,dataTypesCasename);
									pro::storage::DeleteObjectAllVersions(storageServerPath,creatorFileName);
									success := 1;
								endif;
							else
								bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"file:/");
								if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then
									! Case in project folder
									caseUrl := FormatString("%s/%s.data", LocalCaseDirectory, bareCasename);
									if ( FileDelete(caseUrl) ) then
										success := 1;
									endif;
								endif;
							endif;
						
							if ( success ) then
								statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('OK');
						
								RefreshAllCases;
								if ( CurrentCasePath = caseName ) then
									SetActiveCase("",statusCode,statusDescription);
								endif;
						
								if ( statusCode = ReturnStatusCode('OK') ) then
									statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' successfully deleted.", statusCode, casename);
								endif;
							else
								statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('ERROR');
								statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' could not be deleted!", statusCode, casename);
							endif;
						
						else
						
							statusCode := ReturnStatusCode('OK');
							statusDescription := FormatString("%i: Case '%s' is already deleted or does not exist.", statusCode, casename);
						
						endif;
						
						return success;
					}
					Parameter statusCode {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter statusDescription {
						Property: Output;
					}
					StringParameter creatorFileName;
					StringParameter casename {
						Property: Input;
					}
					StringParameter bareCasename;
					StringParameter dataTypesCasename;
					StringParameter storageServerPath;
					Parameter len;
					StringParameter caseUrl;
					Parameter success;
					StringParameter storageType;
				}
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection Interface_Declarations {
			Set AllWebUICases {
				SubsetOf: AllCases;
				Index: IndexWebUICases;
			}
			StringParameter CasePath {
				IndexDomain: (IndexWebUICases);
			}
			Parameter CaseReadOnly {
				IndexDomain: (IndexWebUICases);
				Definition: 1 onlyif ( CaseReadOnlyBool(IndexWebUICases) = "true" );
			}
			StringParameter CaseReadOnlyBool {
				IndexDomain: (IndexWebUICases);
			}
			Parameter CompareCase {
				IndexDomain: (IndexWebUICases);
				Definition: 1 onlyif (( IndexWebUICases = CurrentCase ) or ( CompareCaseBool(IndexWebUICases) = "true" ));
			}
			StringParameter CompareCaseBool {
				IndexDomain: (IndexWebUICases);
			}
			Parameter IsCaseModified {
				IndexDomain: (IndexWebUICases);
				Definition: 1 onlyif (( IndexWebUICases = CurrentCase ) and ( CurrentCaseIsDirty ));
			}
			StringParameter IsCaseModifiedBool {
				IndexDomain: (IndexWebUICases);
				Definition: if ( IsCaseModified(IndexWebUICases) ) then "true" else "false" endif;
			}
			StringParameter IsCaseActiveBool {
				IndexDomain: (IndexWebUICases);
				Definition: if ( IndexWebUICases = CurrentCase ) then "true" else "false" endif;
			}
			StringParameter ComparedCaseAnnotations {
				IndexDomain: IndexCurrentCaseSelection;
				Definition: ActiveCaseAnnotation(IndexCurrentCaseSelection) + CaseReadOnlyAnnotation(IndexCurrentCaseSelection);
			}
			StringParameter CurrentCasePath {
				InitialData: "";
			}
			Parameter CurrentCaseIsDirty {
				InitialData: 0;
			}
			Set CaseFileContentType {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection Case_Annotation_Declarations {
			StringParameter ActiveCaseAnnotation {
				IndexDomain: IndexCurrentCaseSelection;
				Definition: "active-case " onlyif ( IndexCurrentCaseSelection = CurrentCase );
			}
			StringParameter CaseReadOnlyAnnotation {
				IndexDomain: IndexCurrentCaseSelection;
				Definition: "case-readOnly " onlyif CaseReadOnly(IndexCurrentCaseSelection);
			}
		}
		Section Auxiliary_Case_Procedures {
			DeclarationSection Auxiliary_Case_Declarations {
				StringParameter LocalCaseDirectory {
					Default: "";
				}
			}
			Procedure DetermineLocalCaseDirectory {
				Body: {
					OptionGetString("Default_data_folder", LocalCaseDirectory);
					
					if ( (not DirectoryExists(LocalCaseDirectory)) and LocalCaseDirectory = "data" and DirectoryExists("Data") ) then
						LocalCaseDirectory := "Data";
					endif;
					
					if ( not DirectoryExists(LocalCaseDirectory) ) then
						DirectoryCreate(LocalCaseDirectory);
					endif;
				}
			}
			Function GetCaseAccessType {
				Arguments: (storagecp);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					storagetype := SubString(storagecp, 2, 9);
					
					if (storagetype = "UserData") then
						GetCaseAccessType := "private";
					else
						GetCaseAccessType := "shared";
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter storagetype;
				StringParameter storagecp {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function GetCaseStorageServerPath {
				Arguments: (storageType);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					empty GetCaseStorageServerPath;
					
					if ( storageType = "UserData" or storageType = "" /*backwards compatibility*/ ) then
					GetCaseStorageServerPath := FormatString("pro:/UserData/%s/%s/Cases/%s",pro::PROEnvironment,pro::PROUserName,pro::ModelName);
					elseif ( storageType = "PublicData" ) then
					GetCaseStorageServerPath := FormatString("pro:/PublicData/Cases/%s",pro::ModelName);
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter storageType {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function MatchesCasename {
				Arguments: (caseelem,casename);
				Body: {
					casenameLowerCase := StringToLower(casename);
					MatchesCasename := 1 onlyif ( (CasePath(caseelem) = casename) or (CasePath(caseelem) = casenameLowerCase) );
				}
				ElementParameter caseelem {
					Range: AllCases;
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter casename {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter casenameLowerCase;
			}
			Function GetBareCasename {
				Arguments: (casename,protocol);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					empty bareCasename;
					
					casenameLen := StringLength(casename);
					protocolLen := StringLength(protocol);
					casenameLowerCase := StringToLower(casename);
					protocolLowerCase := StringToLower(protocol);
					
					if ( SubString(casenameLowerCase,1,protocolLen) = protocolLowerCase and SubString(casenameLowerCase,casenameLen-4,casenameLen) = ".data" ) then
						numSlashes := StringOccurrences(casename,"/");
						pos := FindNthString(casename,"/",numSlashes);
						BareCasename := SubString(casename,pos+1,casenameLen-5);
					endif;
					
					GetBareCasename := bareCasename;
				}
				StringParameter casename {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter casenameLen;
				Parameter protocolLen;
				StringParameter casenameLowerCase;
				StringParameter protocolLowerCase;
				Parameter numSlashes;
				Parameter pos;
				StringParameter bareCasename;
				StringParameter protocol {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function IsLocalCase {
				Arguments: (casename);
				Body: {
					bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"file:/");
					
					IsLocalCase := if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then 1 else 0 endif;
				}
				StringParameter casename {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter bareCasename;
			}
			Function GetCaseUrl {
				Arguments: (casename);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					empty caseUrl;
					
					bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"pro:/");
					if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then
					  	! PRO Storage case
					   	storageType := GetStorageType(casename);
					   	storageServerPath := GetCaseStorageServerPath(storageType);
					   	caseUrl := FormatString("%s/%s.data",storageServerPath,bareCasename);
					else
					   	bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"file:/");
					   	if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then
					   		! Case in project folder
					   		caseUrl := FormatString("%s/%s.data", LocalCaseDirectory, bareCasename);
					   	endif;
					endif;
					
					GetCaseUrl := caseUrl;
				}
				StringParameter casename {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter bareCasename;
				StringParameter storageServerPath;
				StringParameter caseUrl;
				StringParameter storageType;
			}
			Function GetStorageType {
				Arguments: (casename);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					empty GetStorageType;
					
					casenameLowerCase := StringToLower(casename);
					
					match := "pro:/private";
					len := StringLength(match);
					
					if ( SubString(casenameLowerCase,1,len) = match ) then
						GetStorageType := "UserData";
					else
						match := "pro:/shared/";
						len := StringLength(match);
						if ( SubString(casenameLowerCase,1,len) = match ) then
							GetStorageType := "PublicData";
						endif;
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter casename {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter match;
				Parameter len;
				StringParameter casenameLowerCase;
			}
			Procedure ExistsCase {
				Arguments: (casename);
				Body: {
					empty numberOfVersions;
					
					bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"pro:/");
					if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then
						! PRO Storage case
						bareCaseName := FormatString("%s.data",bareCasename);
						storageType := GetStorageType(casename);
						storageServerPath := GetCaseStorageServerPath(storageType) + "/";
						! strip "pro:" from path
						len := StringLength(storageServerPath);
						storageServerPath := SubString(storageServerPath,5,len-1);
						if ( PROIsInitialized ) then
							pro::storage::GetObjectInfo(storageServerPath,bareCaseName,objFileName,objFileType,numberOfVersions,authorization,lastVersionID);
						else
							numberOfVersions := 0;
						endif;
						logInfo := FormatString("Exists WebUI PRO storage case : (%s,%s) = %n",storageServerPath,bareCaseName,numberOfVersions);
						LocalLogInfo(logInfo);
					else
						bareCasename := GetBareCasename(casename,"file:/");
						if ( StringLength(bareCasename) > 0 ) then
							! Case in project folder
							caseUrl := FormatString("%s/%s.data", LocalCaseDirectory, bareCasename);
							LocalLogInfo("Exists WebUI local case : " + caseUrl);
							numberOfVersions := FileExists(caseUrl);
							logInfo := FormatString("Exists WebUI local case : %s = %n",caseUrl,numberOfVersions);
							LocalLogInfo(logInfo);
						endif;
					endif;
					
					if ( numberOfVersions > 0 ) then
						return 1;
					else
						return 0;
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter casename {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter bareCasename;
				StringParameter storageServerPath;
				StringParameter storageType;
				Parameter len;
				Parameter numberOfVersions;
				StringParameter objFileName;
				StringParameter objFileType;
				StringParameter authorization;
				StringParameter lastVersionID;
				StringParameter logInfo;
				StringParameter caseUrl;
			}
			Procedure ExtendAllCasesFromPROStoragePath {
				Arguments: (storagepath);
				Body: {
					if ( PROIsInitialized ) then
					! strip "pro:" from path
						len := StringLength(storagepath);
						storagepath := SubString(storagepath, 5, len);
					
						pro::management::LocalLogInfo("List WebUI Cases on PRO server in path " + storagepath);
						webui::proint::ListObjectsWithEffectiveAuthorization(storagepath,PROCases,PROCaseName,PROCaseType,PROCaseSize,PROCaseEffectiveAuthorization);
						! filter out all actual cases files and store them in the public set ManagedSessionListedCases
						for (pc) do
							casename := FormatString("%e",pc);
					
							len := StringLength(casename);
							if ( SubString(casename,len-4,len) = ".data" ) then
								caseUrl := FormatString("pro:%s/%e",storagepath,pc);
					
								if (webui::proint::HasReadAccess(PROCaseEffectiveAuthorization(pc))) then
									if(IsValidCase(caseUrl)) then
										Apply(ActualCaseFileURLtoElement, caseUrl, aCase);
										AllWebUICases += aCase;
										accessType := GetCaseAccessType(storagepath);
										CasePath(aCase) := FormatString("pro:/%s/%e",accessType,pc);
										CaseReadOnlyBool(aCase) := if ( webui::proint::HasWriteAccess(PROCaseEffectiveAuthorization(pc)) ) then "false" else "true" endif;
										casecount += 1;
									endif;
								endif;
							endif;
						endfor;
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter storagepath {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter len;
				ElementParameter aCase {
					Range: AllCases;
				}
				StringParameter caseUrl;
				Parameter casecount;
				StringParameter casename;
				StringParameter storageType;
				StringParameter accessType;
				Set PROCases {
					Index: pc;
				}
				StringParameter PROCaseName {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
				StringParameter PROCaseType {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
				Parameter PROCaseSize {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
				StringParameter PROCaseAuthorization {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
				Parameter PROCaseEffectiveAuthorization {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
			}
			Procedure RefreshAllCases {
				Body: {
					empty LocalFileNumbers, casecount, PROCases;
					AllWebUICases := {};
					
					if ( DirectoryExists(LocalCaseDirectory) ) then
						DirectoryGetFiles(LocalCaseDirectory,"*.data",LocalFileNames);
						LocalLogInfo("DirectoryGetFiles (when searching for local/bundled case files): " + StringParameterToJsonList(LocalFileNames(fn)));
					endif;
					
					for (fn) do
						caseUrl := FormatString("%s%s%s", LocalCaseDirectory, pro::FileSeparator, LocalFileNames(fn));
						LocalLogInfo("IsValidCase("+caseUrl+") => "+IsValidCase(caseUrl));
						if(IsValidCase(caseUrl)) then
							CaseFileURLtoElement(caseUrl, aCase);
							AllWebUICases += aCase;
							CasePath(aCase) := FormatString("file:/%s",LocalFileNames(fn));
							CaseReadOnlyBool(aCase) := if ( PROIsInitialized ) then "true" else "false" endif;
						endif;
					endfor;
					
					ExtendAllCasesFromPROStoragePath(GetCaseStorageServerPath("UserData"));
					ExtendAllCasesFromPROStoragePath(GetCaseStorageServerPath("PublicData"));
					
					CompareCaseBool(IndexWebUICases | CompareCaseBool(IndexWebUICases)="") := "false";
				}
				Set LocalFileNumbers {
					SubsetOf: Integers;
					Index: fn;
				}
				StringParameter LocalFileNames {
					IndexDomain: fn;
					InitialData: {
						"";
					}
				}
				StringParameter StoragePath;
				Parameter len;
				ElementParameter aCase {
					Range: AllCases;
				}
				StringParameter caseUrl;
				Parameter casecount;
				Set PROCases {
					Index: pc;
				}
				StringParameter PROCaseName {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
				StringParameter PROCaseType {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
				Parameter PROCaseSize {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
				StringParameter PROCaseAuthorization {
					IndexDomain: pc;
				}
			}
			Procedure IsValidCase {
				Arguments: (caseUrl);
				Body: {
					if(Apply(ActualCaseFileURLtoElement, caseUrl, aCase) and CaseFileGetContentType(caseUrl, contentType)) then
						return 1;
					else
						if ( FindString(CurrentErrorMessage,"Read contents named") and FindString(CurrentErrorMessage,"is not an element in") ) then
							! webui issue 2416
							return 1;
						endif;
						return 0;
					endif;
				}
				ElementParameter contentType {
					Range: AllSubsetsOfAllIdentifiers;
				}
				ElementParameter aCase {
					Range: AllCases;
				}
				StringParameter caseUrl {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Procedure InitializeCurrentCase {
				Body: {
					if ( CurrentCase <> '' ) then
						CurrentCasePath := CasePath(CurrentCase);
					endif;
					CurrentCaseIsDirty := 0;
				}
			}
		}
		Section CubeEngine_Hook_Procedures {
			Parameter CompareCasePrevious {
				IndexDomain: IndexWebUICases;
				InitialData: -1;
			}
			Procedure SyncCompareCaseToCurrentCaseSelection {
				Arguments: (force);
				Body: {
					caseSelectionHasChanged := exists[ IndexCases | CompareCase(IndexCases) <> CompareCasePrevious(IndexCases) ];
					if (force or caseSelectionHasChanged) then
						CurrentCaseSelection := { IndexCases | CompareCase(IndexCases) };
						CompareCasePrevious(IndexWebUICases) := CompareCase(IndexWebUICases);
					endif;
				}
				Parameter force {
					Default: 0;
					Property: Optional;
				}
				Parameter caseSelectionHasChanged;
			}
			Procedure PostRunProcedureHook {
				Body: {
					SyncCompareCaseToCurrentCaseSelection;
					DataChangeMonitorsUpdate;
				}
			}
			Procedure PostRequestSetValueHook {
				Body: {
					SyncCompareCaseToCurrentCaseSelection;
					DataChangeMonitorsUpdate;
				}
			}
		}
		Procedure LocalLogInfo {
			Arguments: (message);
			Body: {
				if ( PROIsInitialized ) then
					pro::management::LocalLogInfo(message);
				endif;
			}
			StringParameter message {
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
		Section State_Management {
			DeclarationSection Temporary_State_Declarations {

					IndexDomain: IndexCases;
				}
				StringParameter Temp_CaseReadOnlyBool {
					IndexDomain: IndexCases;
				}
				StringParameter Temp_CompareCaseBool {
					IndexDomain: IndexCases;
				}

					InitialData: "";

					InitialData: 0;

			DeclarationSection Save_State_Option {
				Parameter UseWebUIState;
