			Procedure SaveDataStateToTemp {
				Body: {
					Temp_CasePath(IndexCases) := CasePath(IndexCases);
					Temp_CaseReadOnlyBool(IndexCases) := CaseReadOnlyBool(IndexCases);
					Temp_CompareCaseBool(IndexCases) := CompareCaseBool(IndexCases);
					Temp_CurrentCasePath := CurrentCasePath;
					Temp_CurrentCaseIsDirty := CurrentCaseIsDirty;
				}
			}
			Procedure LoadDataStateFromTemp {
				Body: {
					SetActiveCase(Temp_CurrentCasePath,statusCode,statusDescription);
					CasePath(IndexCases) := Temp_CasePath(IndexCases);
					CaseReadOnlyBool(IndexCases) := Temp_CaseReadOnlyBool(IndexCases);
					CompareCaseBool(IndexCases) := Temp_CompareCaseBool(IndexCases);
					CurrentCasePath := Temp_CurrentCasePath;
					CurrentCaseIsDirty := Temp_CurrentCaseIsDirty;
				}
				Parameter statusCode;
				StringParameter statusDescription;
			}
			Function GetStateStorageServerFolder {
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					CaseStoragePath := GetCaseStorageServerPath("UserData");
					GetStateStorageServerFolder := FormatString("%s/%s/",CaseStoragePath,pro::ModelVersion);
				}
				StringParameter CaseStoragePath;
			}
			Function GetStateStorageServerPath {
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					StateStorageFolder := GetStateStorageServerFolder;
					GetStateStorageServerPath := FormatString("%swebuistate.data",StateStorageFolder);
				}
				StringParameter StateStorageFolder;
			}
			Procedure ExistsWebUIState {
				Body: {
					if (UseWebUIState and (ExistsWebUIStateHook <> '')) then
						return Apply(ExistsWebUIStateHook);
					else
						return 0;
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter DataSessionID;
				StringParameter StoragePath;
				StringParameter StateName;
				Parameter len;
				Parameter numberOfVersions;
				StringParameter objFileName;
				StringParameter objFileType;
				StringParameter authorization;
				StringParameter lastVersionID;
			}
			Procedure DefaultExistsWebUIState {
				Body: {
					StoragePath := GetStateStorageServerFolder;
					
					! strip "pro:" from path
					len := StringLength(StoragePath);
					StoragePath := SubString(StoragePath,5,len-1);
					
					pro::management::LocalLogInfo("ExistsWebUIState on PRO server : " + StoragePath);
					
					StateName := "webuistate.data";
					pro::storage::GetObjectInfo(StoragePath,StateName,objFileName,objFileType,numberOfVersions,authorization,lastVersionID);
					
					pro::management::LocalLogInfo("ExistsWebUIState sees " + numberOfVersions + " versions");
					
					return numberOfVersions;
				}
				StringParameter StoragePath;
				StringParameter StateName;
				Parameter len;
				Parameter numberOfVersions;
				StringParameter objFileName;
				StringParameter objFileType;
				StringParameter authorization;
				StringParameter lastVersionID;
			}
			Procedure RestoreWebUIState {
				Body: {
					if (UseWebUIState and (RestoreWebUIStateHook <> '')) then
						Apply(RestoreWebUIStateHook);
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter Message;
				StringParameter StoragePath;
				Parameter numberOfVersions;
			}
			Procedure DefaultRestoreWebUIState {
				Body: {
					numberOfVersions := pro::webui::ExistsWebUIState;
					if ( numberOfVersions > 0 ) then
						StoragePath := GetStateStorageServerPath;
						Message := "Restoring WebUI state from '" + StoragePath + "'";
						display Message;
						pro::management::PassMessage(3,Message);
					
						if (not CaseFileMerge(StoragePath)) then
							pro::management::PassMessage(2,CurrentErrorMessage);
						endif;
						LoadDataStateFromTemp;
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter Message;
				StringParameter StoragePath;
				Parameter numberOfVersions;
			}
			Procedure SaveWebUIState {
				Body: {
					if (UseWebUIState and (SaveWebUIStateHook <> '')) then
						Apply(SaveWebUIStateHook);
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter Message;
				StringParameter StoragePath;
			}
			Procedure DefaultSaveWebUIState {
				Body: {
					StoragePath := GetStateStorageServerPath;
					Message := "Saving WebUI state to '" + StoragePath + "'";
					display Message;
					pro::management::PassMessage(3,Message);
					
					SaveDataStateToTemp;
					
					if (not CaseFileSave(StoragePath,AllIdentifiers)) then
						pro::management::PassMessage(2,CurrentErrorMessage);
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter Message;
				StringParameter StoragePath;
			}
		}
		Procedure QuitWebUI {
			Body: {
				DataSessionID := pro::ReadArguments('_pro-webui-session-id');
				
				Message := "Quiting WebUI for session with id " + DataSessionID;
				display Message;
				pro::management::PassMessage(3,Message);
			}
			StringParameter DataSessionID;
			StringParameter Message;
		}
	}
	Section Data_Change_Monitor_Procedures {
		Procedure ResetAllDataChangeMonitors {
			Body: {
				for webui::rdcm do
					DataChangeMonitorReset(webui::rdcm);
				endfor;
			}
			StringParameter strValue;
			Set aMonitoredIdentifierSet {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
		ElementParameter dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary {
			Range: AllIdentifiers;
		}
		Parameter monitorIdCounter {
			InitialData: 0;
		}
		Set RegisteredDataChangeMonitors {
			Index: rdcm;
		}
		ElementParameter MonitoredDataChangedProcedures {
			IndexDomain: rdcm;
			Range: AllProcedures;
			Default: 'webui::NoOp';
		}
		ElementParameter ResetDataChangeMonitorProcedures {
			IndexDomain: rdcm;
			Range: AllProcedures;
			Default: 'webui::NoOp';
		}
		ElementParameter MonitoredIdentifierSets {
			IndexDomain: rdcm;
			Range: AllIdentifiers;
		}
		ElementParameter RegisteredProcedures {
			IndexDomain: rdcm;
			Range: AllProcedures;
			Default: 'webui::NoOp';
		}
		Set tempGlobalMonitoredIdentifiers {
			SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
		}
		Procedure createDataChangeMonitorProcedures {
			Arguments: {
				(monitorId,identifierSet,aMonitoredDataChangedProcedureRef,resetDataChangeMonitorProcedureRef,
				 aMonitoredIdentifierSetRef)
			}
			Body: {
				
				setName := monitorId+"_Set";
				
				/* Makes the function idempotent: */
				/*
				me::Delete('DataChangeMonitorRuntime');
				block
					DataChangeMonitorDelete(monitorId);
				onerror err do
				    	errh::MarkAsHandled(err);
				endblock;
				dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary := me::CreateLibrary("DataChangeMonitorRuntime", "dcmr");
				*/
				
				dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary := StringToElement(AllIdentifiers, "DataChangeMonitorRuntime", 0);
				if not dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary then
					 dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary := me::CreateLibrary("DataChangeMonitorRuntime", "dcmr");
					 me::SetAttribute(dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary, 'property', "NoSave");
					 me::Compile(dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary);
				endif;
				
				! Create the runtime Set
				aMonitoredIdentifierSetRef := me::Create(setName, 'set', dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary, 0);
				me::SetAttribute(aMonitoredIdentifierSetRef, 'subset of', "AllIdentifiers");
				me::Compile(aMonitoredIdentifierSetRef);
				
				! Create the data monitor
				createMonitorProcedureRef := me::Create("CreateDataChangeMonitor", 'procedure', dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary, 0);
				tempGlobalMonitoredIdentifiers := identifierSet;
				me::SetAttribute(createMonitorProcedureRef, 'body',
					setName+" := webui::tempGlobalMonitoredIdentifiers;\n"+
					"DataChangeMonitorCreate(\""+monitorId+"\", "+setName+", 0);\n"
				);
				me::Compile(createMonitorProcedureRef);
				Apply(createMonitorProcedureRef);
				me::Delete(createMonitorProcedureRef);
				
				! Create data changed procedure ref
				aMonitoredDataChangedProcedureRef := me::Create("MonitoredDataChangedProcedure_for_"+setName, 'procedure', dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary, 0);
				me::SetAttribute(aMonitoredDataChangedProcedureRef, 'body',
				!	"DialogMessage(FormatString(\"hasChanged("+monitorId+") = %n\",DataChangeMonitorHasChanged(\""+monitorId+"\")));\n"+
					"if ( DataChangeMonitorHasChanged(\""+monitorId+"\") = 1 ) then\n"+
					"\tDataChangeMonitorReset(\""+monitorId+"\", "+setName+");\n"+
					"\treturn 1;\n"+
					"else\n"+
					"\treturn 0;\n"+
					"endif;\n"
				);
				me::Compile(aMonitoredDataChangedProcedureRef);
				
				
				! Create data changed procedure ref
				resetDataChangeMonitorProcedureRef := me::Create("ResetDataChangeMonitor_for_"+setName, 'procedure', dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary, 0);
				me::SetAttribute(resetDataChangeMonitorProcedureRef, 'body',
					"DataChangeMonitorReset(\""+monitorId+"\", "+setName+");\n"
				);
				me::Compile(resetDataChangeMonitorProcedureRef);
				
				me::Compile(dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary);
			}
			ElementParameter createMonitorProcedureRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Default: 'webui::NoOp';
			}
			StringParameter setName;
			ElementParameter err {
				Range: errh::PendingErrors;
			}
			Set identifierSet {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter monitorId {
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter aMonitoredDataChangedProcedureRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Output;
			}
			ElementParameter aMonitoredIdentifierSetRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Output;
			}
			ElementParameter resetDataChangeMonitorProcedureRef {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Output;
			}
		}
		Procedure DataChangeMonitorRegisterCallback {
			Arguments: (identifierSet,callback,aMonitor);
			Body: {
				argumentsToString:= AttributeToString(callback,'arguments');
				
				if argumentsToString = "" or argumentsToString = "Encrypted" then
					monitorIdCounter := monitorIdCounter + 1;
				
					monitorId := FormatString("DataChangeMonitor%03i", monitorIdCounter);
				
					block
						DataChangeMonitorDelete(monitorId);
					onerror err do
						errh::MarkAsHandled(err);
					endblock;
				
					aMonitor := StringToElement(RegisteredDataChangeMonitors,monitorId,1);
					RegisteredProcedures(aMonitor) := callback;
					createDataChangeMonitorProcedures(monitorId, identifierSet, MonitoredDataChangedProcedures(aMonitor), ResetDataChangeMonitorProcedures(aMonitor), MonitoredIdentifierSets(aMonitor));
				else
					raise error "Second argument of DataChangeMonitorRegisterCallback should be a procedure that doesn't accept arguments : '" + FormatString("%e'", callback) ;
				endif;
			}
			StringParameter argumentsToString;
			Set identifierSet2 {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter err {
				Range: errh::PendingErrors;
			}
			Set identifierSet {
				SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
				Property: Input;
			}
			StringParameter monitorId;
			ElementParameter callback {
				Range: AllProcedures;
				Property: Input;
			}
			ElementParameter aMonitor {
				Range: RegisteredDataChangeMonitors;
				Property: Output;
			}
		}
		Procedure DataChangeMonitorUnregisterCallback {
			Arguments: (aMonitor);
			Body: {
				me::Delete(MonitoredDataChangedProcedures(aMonitor));
				me::Delete(ResetDataChangeMonitorProcedures(aMonitor));
				me::Delete(MonitoredIdentifierSets(aMonitor));
				RegisteredProcedures(aMonitor) := {};
				RegisteredDataChangeMonitors -= aMonitor;
			}
			ElementParameter aMonitor {
				Range: RegisteredDataChangeMonitors;
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
		Procedure DataChangeMonitorReset {
			Arguments: (aMonitor);
			Body: {
				!DataChangeMonitorReset(FormatString("%e", aMonitor), MonitoredIdentifierSets(aMonitor));
				if(ResetDataChangeMonitorProcedures(aMonitor)) then
					Apply(ResetDataChangeMonitorProcedures(aMonitor));
				else
					raise error "DataChangeMonitorReset: No such monitor: " + aMonitor;
				endif;
			}
			ElementParameter aMonitor {
				Range: RegisteredDataChangeMonitors;
				Property: Input;
			}
		}
		Procedure DataChangeMonitorsUpdate {
			Body: {
				TemporaryRegisteredDataChangeMonitors := RegisteredDataChangeMonitors;
				
				AnyDataChange := 1;
				
				! Get sure runtime libraries are compiled before calling procedures in them
				webuiRuntimeLibrary := StringToElement(AllIdentifiers, "AimmsWebUIRuntime");
				if (webuiRuntimeLibrary <> '') then
					me::Compile(webuiRuntimeLibrary);
				endif;
				
				dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary := StringToElement(AllIdentifiers, "DataChangeMonitorRuntime");
				if (dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary <> '') then
					me::Compile(dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary);
				endif;
				
				while (AnyDataChange and (loopCount <= 5)) do
					AnyDataChange := 0;
					block where warning_set_order_changes_during_for_statement := 'off';
						for (trdcm|MonitoredDataChangedProcedures(trdcm)) do
							if ( Apply(MonitoredDataChangedProcedures(trdcm)) = 1 ) then
								AnyDataChange := 1;
								LocalLogInfo("DataChangeMonitorsUpdate: Applying RegisteredProcedure=" + RegisteredProcedures(trdcm));
								Apply(RegisteredProcedures(trdcm));
							endif;
						endfor;
					endblock;
				endwhile;
			}
			Set TemporaryRegisteredDataChangeMonitors {
				SubsetOf: RegisteredDataChangeMonitors;
				Index: trdcm;
			}
			Index rdcm {
				Range: RegisteredDataChangeMonitors;
			}
			Parameter AnyDataChange;
			ElementParameter webuiRuntimeLibrary {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
			ElementParameter dataChangeMonitorRuntimeLibrary {
				Range: AllIdentifiers;
			}
		}
	}

	Procedure PROIsInitialized {
		Body: {
			if pro::GetPROEndPoint() then
				block
					isPROInitialized := pro::management::IsInitialized;
				onerror err do
					isPROInitialized := 0;
					errh::MarkAsHandled(err);
				endblock;
			else
				isPROInitialized := 0;
			endif;
			
			return isPROInitialized;
		}
		ElementParameter err {
			Range: errh::PendingErrors;
		}
		Parameter isPROInitialized;
	}
	Procedure ParseCommandlineArguments {
		Body: {
			! Read session command-line arguments (pair key-value)
			CLIdx := 1;
			empty ReadParameters;
			WHILE CLIdx <= 50 and SessionArgument(CLIdx,CLArg) = 1 DO
					CommandLineArgument := StringToElement(CommandLineArguments,CLArg);
					IF CommandLineArgument <> '' THEN
							! If it is a known key, check the value (next argument)
							CLIdx += 1;
							IF SessionArgument(CLIdx,CLArg) = 0 THEN
									! No value informed for this key; assign "1"
									ReadParameters(CommandLineArgument) := "1";
									CLIdx -= 1;
							ELSEIF SubString(CLArg,1,6) = "webui_" THEN
									! The next argument is also a key; no value informed for this one, assign "1"
									ReadParameters(CommandLineArgument) := "1";
									CLIdx -= 1;
							ELSE
									! Interpret key and value
									ReadParameters(CommandLineArgument) := CLArg;
							ENDIF;
					ENDIF;
					! Check next pair (or next argument, in the case the last one was a invalid key)
					CLIdx += 1;
			ENDWHILE;
		}
		ElementParameter CommandLineArgument {
			Range: CommandLineArguments;
		}
		Parameter CLIdx {
			Range: integer;
			InitialData: 0;
		}
		StringParameter CLArg;
	}
	Procedure LibraryInitialization {
		Body: {
			ParseCommandlineArguments;
			
			CaseFileContentType := AllIdentifiers;
			IsProjectDeveloperMode := ProjectDeveloperMode();
			DataSessionID := pro::ReadArguments('_pro-webui-session-id');
			DataSessionUser := pro::ReadArguments('_pro-username');
			if (not AimmsRevisionString(webui::AimmsVersion,4)) then
				webui::AimmsVersion := "unable to determine Aimms Version";
			endif;
			
			if (PROIsInitialized) then
				pro::management::RetrieveAccountInfo(AccountInfoAttributes,AccountInfoValues);
				DataSessionDNS := AccountInfoValues('DNS_NAME');
			endif;
			
			SessionType := ReadParameters('_pro-session-type');
			if (SessionType = "") then
				SessionType := "developer";
			endif;
			
			StartServer(ReadParameters);
			
			UseWebUIState := 0;
			if ( OptionGetString("Save_WebUI_State", UseWebUIStateOptionValue) ) then
				if ( UseWebUIStateOptionValue <> "Never" ) then
					UseWebUIState := 1;
				endif;
			endif;
			
			InitializeAnnotations;
			InitializeTimeZoneData;
			
			if (IsProjectDeveloperMode) then
				CheckForOldStyleAnnotations;
			endif;
			
			DetermineLocalCaseDirectory;
			GenerateAllPublicIdentifiersSet;
			
			if ( WebUIDLLPresent ) then
				GetAllPages;
			endif;
		}
		StringParameter UseWebUIStateOptionValue;
		Set AccountInfoAttributes {
			Index: aia;
			InitialData: data { 'DNS_NAME' };
		}
		StringParameter AccountInfoValues {
			IndexDomain: aia;
		}
	}
	Procedure LibraryTermination {
		Body: {
			if ( WebUIDLLPresent ) then
				StopServer;
			endif;
			return 1 ;
		}
	}
	Procedure PreLibraryTermination {
		Body: {
			return 1 ;
		}
	}
	Procedure PostLibraryInitialization {
		Body: {
			if (AimmsStringConstants('Platform') = "Windows" AND PROIsInitialized AND DirectoryExists("MainProject\\WebUI")=1) then
				Execute("cmd", "/c \"mklink /j WebUI MainProject\\WebUI\"", wait: 1, minimized : 1);
			endif;
			if (AimmsStringConstants('Platform') = "Linux" AND PROIsInitialized AND DirectoryExists("MainProject/WebUI")=1) then
				Execute("bash", "-c \"ln -s MainProject/WebUI WebUI\"", wait: 1, minimized : 1);
			endif;
			
			empty CompareCaseBool;
			
			IF PROIsInitialized THEN
				RestoreWebUIState;
			ENDIF;
			
			CompareCaseBoolSet := data {CompareCaseBool};
			DataChangeMonitorCreate("CompareCaseDataChangeMonitor", CompareCaseBoolSet);
			
			RefreshAllCases;
			InitializeCurrentCase;
			
			InitializeModelWebUIInteraction;
			
			empty Requests;
			empty RequestQueue;
			
			SyncCompareCaseToCurrentCaseSelection(1);
			
			DataChangeMonitorsUpdate;
		}
		Set CompareCaseBoolSet {
			SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
		}
	}
	Section WebUI_Frontend_State_Support {
		Section Public_WebUI_Frontend_State_Support_Procedures {
			Procedure MarkActiveTab {
				Arguments: (tabId);
				Body: {
					SetElementAdd(AllOpenWebUITabs, TmpTab, tabId);
					
					Now := StringToMoment("%c%y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", [s], "2000-01-01 00:00:00", CurrentToString("%c%y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"));
					
					/* Update the timestamp at which the ping for this tab was received */
					TabPingTimeStamp(TmpTab) := Now;
					
					/* Remove all tabs from the set which have a timestamp older than 30 seconds before this last added timestamp */
					empty TabsToRemove;
					
					TabsToRemove := { indexAllOpenWebUITabs | (Now - TabPingTimeStamp(indexAllOpenWebUITabs)) > 60[s] };
					
					/* All tabs to remove have been identified, now actually remove them */
					AllOpenWebUITabs -= TabsToRemove;
				}
				Comment: "This procedure is invoked when a tab marks that it is still active.";
				StringParameter tabId {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter Now {
					Unit: s;
				}
				Set TabsToRemove {
					SubsetOf: AllOpenWebUITabs;
				}
			}
			Procedure RemoveActiveTab {
				Arguments: (tabId);
				Body: {
					AllOpenWebUITabs -= StringToElement(AllOpenWebUITabs, tabId);
				}
				Comment: "This procedure is invoked when a tab marks that it is still active.";
				StringParameter tabId {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
		}
		DeclarationSection Public_WebUI_Frontend_State_Support_Declarations {
			Set AllOpenWebUITabs {
				Index: indexAllOpenWebUITabs;
				Parameter: TmpTab;
				Comment: "This set contains all WebUI tabs currently open in the browser. Note: If a connection between the browser and the backend is lost, it may take a view moments for this tab to disappear from this list.";
			}
			ElementParameter LastActiveWebUITab {
				Range: AllOpenWebUITabs;
				Definition: {
					ArgMax(indexAllOpenWebUITabs, TabUserInteractionTimeStamp(indexAllOpenWebUITabs));
				}
				Comment: "An element parameter ranged over the AllOpenWebUITabs set, used to indicate the last WebUI tab on which user interaction was recorded.";
			}
			ElementParameter CurrentPageId {
				IndexDomain: indexAllOpenWebUITabs;
				Range: AllRegularPages;
				Comment: "This element parameter contains the PageIds of Regular page\'s for all open tabs in the WebUI.";
			}
			ElementParameter CurrentSidePanelPageId {
				IndexDomain: indexAllOpenWebUITabs;
				Range: AllSidePanelPages;
				Comment: "This element parameter contains the SidePanel PageId for the currently opened sidepanel tab in the WebUI.";
			}
			Parameter TabPingTimeStamp {
				IndexDomain: indexAllOpenWebUITabs;
				Unit: s;
			}
			Parameter TabUserInteractionTimeStamp {
				IndexDomain: indexAllOpenWebUITabs;
			}
		}
	}
	Section Utilities {
		Section Identifier_utilities {
			Function IsAKindOfSet {
				Arguments: (idRef);
				Body: {
					idType := IdentifierType(idRef);
					if ( ( idType = 'set' ) or ( idType = 'horizon' ) or ( idType = 'calendar' ) ) then
						IsAKindOfSet := 1;
					else
						IsAKindOfSet := 0;
					endif;
				}
				ElementParameter idRef {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter idType {
					Range: AllIdentifierTypes;
				}
			}
		}
		Section MEF_utilities {
			Procedure CreateIdentifierIfNotPresent {
				Arguments: (identifierName,identifierType,theSection);
				Body: {
					theCurrentSection := theSection;
					libraryPrefix := me::GetAttribute(theCurrentSection, 'prefix');
					
					while( not libraryPrefix and me::Parent(theCurrentSection)) do
						theCurrentSection := me::Parent(theCurrentSection);
						libraryPrefix := me::GetAttribute(theCurrentSection, 'prefix');
					endwhile;
					
					if ( not StringToElement(AllIdentifiers, libraryPrefix + "::" + identifierName) ) then
						aRuntimeIdentifierRef := me::Create(identifierName, identifierType, theSection, 0);
					
						if ( identifierType = "string parameter" ) then
							me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'initial data', "");
						elseif ( identifierType = "parameter" ) then
							me::SetAttribute(aRuntimeIdentifierRef,'initial data', "0");
						endif;
					endif;
				}
				StringParameter libraryPrefix;
				ElementParameter aRuntimeIdentifierRef {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
				}
				StringParameter identifierName {
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter identifierType {
					Range: AllIdentifierTypes;
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter theSection {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter theCurrentSection {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
				}
			}
			Procedure GetOrCreateWebUIRuntimeLibrary {
				Arguments: (lib);
				Body: {
					lib := StringToElement(AllSymbols, "AimmsWebUIRuntime", 0);
					if not lib then
						lib := me::CreateLibrary("AimmsWebUIRuntime", "webui_runtime");
					 	me::SetAttribute(lib, 'property', "NoSave");
					 	me::Compile(lib);
					endif;
				}
				ElementParameter lib {
					Range: AllSymbols;
					Property: Output;
				}
			}
			Procedure GetRuntimeIdentifierRef {
				Arguments: (identifierName,identifierRef);
				Body: {
					identifierRef := StringToElement(AllIdentifiers, "webui_runtime::" + identifierName);
				}
				StringParameter identifierName {
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter identifierRef {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Property: Output;
				}
			}
			Procedure CreateRuntimeProcedure {
				Arguments: (procedureName,procedureBody,theSection,procedureIdentifier);
				Body: {
					procedureIdentifier := me::Create(procedureName,'procedure',theSection);
					me::SetAttribute(procedureIdentifier,'body',procedureBody);
				}
				StringParameter procedureName {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter procedureBody {
					Property: Input;
				}
				ElementParameter procedureIdentifier {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Property: Output;
				}
				ElementParameter theWebUIRuntimeLibrary {
					Range: AllSymbols;
				}
				ElementParameter theSection {
					Range: AllIdentifiers;
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
		}
		Section Json_utilities {
			Function toJsonString {
				Arguments: (str);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					! escape string
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\\","\\\\");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\"","\\\"");
					
					! Assuming that calling 32 C-functions is faster
					! than doing interpreted string magic in AIMMS using a for-loop
					! (Ultimately we want a regex replace in C)
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\000","\\u0000");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\001","\\u0001");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\002","\\u0002");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\003","\\u0003");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\004","\\u0004");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\005","\\u0005");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\006","\\u0006");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\007","\\u0007");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\008","\\u0008");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\009","\\u0009");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\00A","\\u000A");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\00B","\\u000B");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\00C","\\u000C");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\00D","\\u000D");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\00E","\\u000E");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\00F","\\u000F");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\010","\\u0010");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\011","\\u0011");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\012","\\u0012");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\013","\\u0013");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\014","\\u0014");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\015","\\u0015");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\016","\\u0016");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\017","\\u0017");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\018","\\u0018");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\019","\\u0019");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\01A","\\u001A");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\01B","\\u001B");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\01C","\\u001C");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\01D","\\u001D");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\01E","\\u001E");
					str := FindReplaceStrings(str,"\01F","\\u001F");
					
					str := FormatString("\"%s\"", str);
					toJsonString := str;
				}
				StringParameter str {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function SettoJsonList {
				Arguments: (aSet);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					tmpString += "[";
					
					for(s) do
						tmpString += toJsonString(FormatString("%e", s));
						if (s <> Last(aSet)) then
							tmpString += ",";
							if ( StringLength(tmpString) > 1900 ) then
								tmpString += "\"<WEBUI SET ELEMENT OVERFLOW>\"";
								break;
							endif;
						endif;
					endfor;
					
					tmpString += "]";
					
					SettoJsonList := tmpString;
				}
				StringParameter tmpString;
				Set aSet {
					Index: s;
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function StringParameterToJsonList {
				Arguments: (aStringParameter);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					tmpString += "[";
					
					for(s) do
						tmpString += toJsonString(FormatString("%s", aStringParameter(s)));
						if (s <> Last(aSet)) then
							tmpString += ",";
							if ( StringLength(tmpString) > 2000 ) then
								tmpString += "<WEBUI SET ELEMENT OVERFLOW>";
								break;
							endif;
						endif;
					endfor;
					
					tmpString += "]";
					
					StringParameterToJsonList := tmpString;
				}
				StringParameter tmpString;
				Set aSet {
					Index: s;
				}
				StringParameter aStringParameter {
					IndexDomain: s;
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
		}
		Section Template_utilities {
			Function ExpandTemplate {
				Arguments: (theCollection,forEachTemplate);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					resultString := "";
					
					for(i) do
						tmpString := FindReplaceStrings(forEachTemplate, "%e", i);
						formIdentifierString := FormatString("%e",i);
						formIdentifierString := FindReplaceStrings(formIdentifierString, "::", "_");
						tmpString := FindReplaceStrings(forEachTemplate, "%i", formIdentifierString);
						resultString += FindReplaceStrings(tmpString, "%e", i);
					endfor;
					
					ExpandTemplate := resultString;
				}
				StringParameter resultString;
				StringParameter formIdentifierString;
				StringParameter tmpString;
				StringParameter forEachTemplate {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Set theCollection {
					Index: i;
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
		}
		Section String_utilities {
			Parameter END_OF_STRING {
				Definition: 999999;
			}
			Function toString {
				Arguments: (a,radix);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					res := "";
					
					! only works for integers, not floating point numbers:
					a := Floor(a);
					while (1) do
						res := charAt(radixDigits, Floor(Mod(a, radix))+1) + res;
						a := Floor(a / radix);
						if ( a = 0 ) then
							break;
						endif;
					endwhile;
					
					toString := res;
				}
				Comment: "returns the string representation of a in the given radix or base 10 if not specified";
				Parameter a {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter radix {
					Default: 10;
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter radixDigits {
					Default: "0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";
				}
				StringParameter res;
				Parameter debug;
			}
			Function charAt {
				Arguments: (a,n);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					charAt := SubString(a, n, n);
				}
				Comment: "returns the n-th character in a as a string";
				StringParameter a {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter n {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function StartsWith {
				Arguments: (str,strToFind);
				Range: integer;
				Body: {
					StartsWith := FindString(str, strToFind, 0) = 1;
				}
				StringParameter str {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter strToFind {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Function JoinNonEmpty {
				Arguments: (separatorString,arg0,arg1,arg2,arg3,arg4);
				Range: string;
				Body: {
					tmpString := "";
					joinString := "";
					
					if(arg0 <> "") then
						tmpString += arg0;
						joinString := separatorString;
					endif;
					
					if(arg1 <> "") then
						tmpString += joinString;
						tmpString += arg1;
						joinString := separatorString;
					endif;
					
					if(arg2 <> "") then
						tmpString += joinString;
						tmpString += arg2;
						joinString := separatorString;
					endif;
					
					if(arg3 <> "") then
						tmpString += joinString;
						tmpString += arg3;
						joinString := separatorString;
					endif;
					
					if(arg4 <> "") then
						tmpString += joinString;
						tmpString += arg4;
						joinString := separatorString;
					endif;
					
					JoinNonEmpty := tmpString;
				}
				Comment: "JoinNonEmpty(\",\",\"a\",\"b\",\"\",\"c\") -> \"a,b,c\"";
				StringParameter separatorString {
					Property: Input;
				}
				StringParameter arg0 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg1 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg2 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg3 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter arg4 {
					Property: Optional;
				}
				StringParameter tmpString;
				StringParameter joinString;
			}
		}
		Section NoOps {
			Procedure NoOp {
				Body: {
					!Does nothing
				}
			}
			Procedure NoOp1 {
				Arguments: (arg1);
				Body: {
					!Does nothing
				}
				StringParameter arg1 {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
			Procedure NoOp3 {
				Arguments: (arg1,arg2,arg3);
				Body: {
					!Does nothing
				}
				StringParameter arg1 {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter arg2 {
					Property: Output;
				}
				StringParameter arg3 {
					Property: Output;
				}
			}
		}
		Section Bitwise_operators {
			Procedure bAND {
				Arguments: (a,b);
				Body: {
					res := 0;
					
					j := 1;
					for (i in {1 .. 8}) do
						res += Floor(Mod(a/j, 2)) * Floor(Mod(b/j, 2)) * j;
						j *= 2;
					endfor;
					
					return res;
				}
				Comment: "returns the bitwise-AND: a & b";
				Parameter a {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter b {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter res;
				Index i {
					Range: Integers;
				}
				Parameter j;
			}
			Procedure bOR {
				Arguments: (a,b);
				Body: {
					res := 0;
					
					j := 1;
					for (i in {1 .. 8}) do
						res += (Floor(Mod(a/j, 2)) + Floor(Mod(b/j, 2)) > 0) * j;
						j *= 2;
					endfor;
					
					return res;
				}
				Comment: "returns the bitwise-OR: a | b";
				Parameter a {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter b {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter res;
				Index i {
					Range: Integers;
				}
				Parameter j;
			}
			Procedure bit {
				Arguments: (a,n);
				Body: {
					return Floor(Mod(a/(2^(n-1)), 2));
				}
				Comment: "returns the n-th bit in a";
				Parameter a {
					Property: Input;
				}
				Parameter n {
					Property: Input;
				}
			}
		}
	}
}


LibraryModule AimmsWebUIRuntime {
	Prefix: webui_runtime;
	Property: NoSave;
	Set AllImplicitPublicIdentifiers {
		SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
	}
	Set AllDefaultPublicIdentifiers {
		SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
	}
	Set AllAllowedIdentifierTypes {
		SubsetOf: AllIdentifierTypes;
		Definition: {
			data { set, calendar, horizon, index, parameter, 'element parameter',
			    'string parameter', variable, 'element variable', 'complementarity variable',
			    constraint, arc, node, 'uncertainty variable', 'uncertainty constraint',
			    procedure, function
			}
		}
	}
	Set AllExplicitPublicIdentifiers {
		SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
		Definition: AllDefaultPublicIdentifiers;
	}
	Set AllActualPublicIdentifiers {
		SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
		Definition: webui_runtime::AllImplicitPublicIdentifiers + webui_runtime::AllExplicitPublicIdentifiers;
	}
	Procedure InitializeAllDefaultPublicIdentifiers {
		Body: {
			block where warning_explicit_element_not_in_set:=off;
			AllDefaultPublicIdentifiers := {IndexIdentifiers | IdentifierType(IndexIdentifiers) in AllAllowedIdentifierTypes}
			 - SectionIdentifiers('AIMMSWebUI')
			 - SectionIdentifiers('AIMMSWebUIRuntime')
			 - if 'AIMMSProLibrary' in AllSections then SectionIdentifiers('AIMMSProLibrary') else {} endif
			 - if 'AimmsProGUI' in AllSections then SectionIdentifiers('AimmsProGUI') else {} endif;
			endblock;
		}
	}
}


LibraryModule DataChangeMonitorRuntime {
	Prefix: dcmr;
	Property: NoSave;
	Set DataChangeMonitor001_Set {
		SubsetOf: AllIdentifiers;
	}
	Procedure MonitoredDataChangedProcedure_for_DataChangeMonitor001_Set {
		Body: {
			if ( DataChangeMonitorHasChanged("DataChangeMonitor001") = 1 ) then
				DataChangeMonitorReset("DataChangeMonitor001", DataChangeMonitor001_Set);
				return 1;
			else
				return 0;
			endif;
		}
	}
	Procedure ResetDataChangeMonitor_for_DataChangeMonitor001_Set {
		Body: {
			DataChangeMonitorReset("DataChangeMonitor001", DataChangeMonitor001_Set);
		}
	}
}
